patippuri
19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutippakutiyum, 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkappakutiyum tamiḻppulamiyum tamiḻāyvum mēlōṅki vaḷarnta poṟkālamākum. ip poṟkālap pakutiyiltāṉ tamiḻppēravic cemmal peruntamiḻaṟiñar ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār tōṉṟi vaḷarntu vāḻntu tāymoḻit tamiḻukku vaḷamum valimiyum cērttār.
ipperum pēraṟiñar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum veḷiyiṭa muṭivuceytu ka.veḷḷivāraṇaṉār nūl varici eṉum talippil 21 tokutikaḷ mutal kaṭṭamāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. kikkuk kiṭikkap peṟāta nūlkaḷit tēṭiyeṭuttu iṉivarum kālaṅkaḷil veḷiyiṭa muyalvōm. 
 tamiḻ ici, tamiḻ ilakkiyam, tamiḻ ilakkaṇam, civa cittāntam ākiya nālvakit tuṟikaḷi miyamākak koṇṭu ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum perum payaṉtarak kūṭiya aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷākum. āḻnta camayappaṟṟāḷar, patavikkum pukaḻukkum kācukkum tammi āṭpaṭuttik koḷḷātu tamiḻppaṇi oṉṟiyē tam vāḻviṉ mūccākak koṇṭu vāḻntavar,  naṭuvaṇaracu tamiḻakattil kalavimoḻiyām intiyik (1938) kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākat tamiḻnāṭṭuk kalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil pukuttiyapōtu ataṉi etirttup pōrpparaṇi pāṭiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷil ivarum oruvar.  kākki viṭutūtu eṉum inti etirppu nūli eḻuti aṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭu āṭcik kaṭṭilil mutalvarāka amarntirunta irācācikku aṉuppit tam etirppip pativu ceytavar. 
tammuṭiya tamiḻāyvuppaṇi mūlam tamiḻ varalāṟṟil nilittu niṟpavar,  tamiḻiyum civattiyum iru kaṇkaḷeṉak koṇṭavar.   tamiḻilakkaṇat toṉṉūlām tolkāppiyatti  yum, piṉṉūlām naṉṉūliyum āḻntakaṉṟu kaṟṟu oppāyvu ceytavar, tam karuttukaḷum vāḻkki muṟiyum muraṇpaṭāmal eṇṇiyatic colli, colliyapaṭi naṭimuṟiyil vāḻntu kāṭṭiya peruntamiḻaṟiñar.  
tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟa peyar iyaṟpeyarē eṉṟu niṟuviyavar, tolkāppiyar kālattil vaṭakkē vēṅkaṭamalittoṭarum, teṟkē kumariyāṟum tamiḻaka ellikaḷāka amintiruntaṉaveṉṟum, kaṭalkōḷukkup piṟaku kumarikkaṭal teṉ elli āṉatu eṉpatiyum, tolkāppiyar iṭiccaṅkak kālattavar, tolkāppiyam iṭiccaṅkak kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, muccaṅka varalāṟṟi mutaṉmutalil kūṟiyatu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uritāṉ eṉpatiyum, tolkāppiyam, caṅkac ceyyuḷukkum tirukkuṟaḷukkum neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉṉarē iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, tirumūlar tam tirumantiramē cittānta cāttiram patiṉāṉkiṟkum mutal nūlāka tikaḻvatu eṉpatiyum, tirumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉ mutalām irācarācaṉ alla mutalām ātittaṉē  tirumuṟi kaṇṭa cōḻaṉ eṉpatiyum,  vaḷḷalāriṉ tiruvaruṭpā tamiḻiṉ colvaḷamum, poruḷ nuṭpamum, oppaṟṟap pēraruḷiṉ iṉpamum niṟintatu  eṉpatiyum, civa camayam āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpatiyum, paḻantamiḻ nākarikattiṉ ūṟṟukkaṇ tamiḻum civamum eṉpatiyum tam nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi uṟuti ceytavar. tam āyvup pulamiyāl pala putiya ceytikaḷiyum tamiḻ ulakukku aḷittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻulakiṟkup perumi cērppaṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟiṟku  kūṭutal varavāka amivaṉa. ivaruṭiya aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷa ṉittiyum āvaṇappaṭuttum   nōkkuṭaṉ tokuttu tamiḻ ulakiṟku vippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. itaṉi veḷikkoṇara emakkut tuṇiyāyirunta em patippakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ, nūlkaḷ koṭuttu utaviyavarkaḷ,  kaṇiṉi, meyppu, accu, nūl kaṭṭamippu ceytu innūlvarici ceppamuṭaṉ  veḷivarat tuṇiniṉṟa aṉivarukkum naṉṟi.  em tamiḻk kāppup paṇikku tuṇi niṟka vēṇṭukiṟōm.
2010	 patippakattār












mutal patippuri
aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattil 1935 mutal 1937 vari yāṉ tamiḻārāycci māṇavaṉāka iruntapoḻutu iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇa nūlākiya tolkāppiyattilum ataṉ vaḻi nūlākiya naṉṉūlilum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷa eḻuttilakkaṇa vitikaḷi oppunōkki ārāyntu tolkāppiyam - naṉṉūl - eḻuttatikāram eṉṉum ārāycciyuriyiṉi eḻutum paṇiyiṉi eṉ pērāciriyap peruntaki nāvalar ṭākṭar ca.cōmacuntara pāratiyār avarkaḷ eṉakku aṉpuṭaṉ vaḻaṅ kiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu eḻutappeṟṟa tolkāppiyam - naṉṉūl - eḻuttatikāra vurip pakuti aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattuṇaril 1941-mutal toṭarntu veḷivantatu. avvurip pakuti 1962-l aṇṇāmalippalkalik kaḻakattiṉ cārpil nūluruvāka veḷivantatu.
ataṉit toṭarntu tolkāppiyac collatikārat tiyum naṉṉūṟ collatikārattiyum oppiṭṭu ārāyntu veḷiyiṭutal vēṇṭum eṉa aṉpar palarum virumpiyataṟku ēṟpa avvappoḻutu tokutta kuṟippukkaḷi aṭi yoṟṟit tolkāppiyam - naṉṉūl - collatikāram eṉṉum ivvuri nūl veḷiyiṭap peṟukiṉṟatu.
acciṭutaṟku ēṟṟa vaṇṇam innūlip paṭiyeṭukkum paṇi eṉ makaḷ celvi. tiru.maṅkiyarkkaraciyāl tirutta muṟac ceyyappeṟṟatu. aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻt tuṟi virivuriyāḷar ṭākṭar cō.na.kantacāmi M.A.,ph.D. avarkaḷ innūliṉ tiruttappaṭikaḷi oppu nōkkik tiruttiyuta viṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷukku iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷāl ellā nalaṅkaḷum uḷavātal vēṇṭum eṉa uḷamāra vāḻttu kiṉṟēṉ.
maturip palkalikkaḻakat tuṇivēntar pērāciriyar ṭākṭar mu.varatarācaṉār avarkaḷ tamakkuḷḷa palvēṟu aluvalkaḷukkiṭiyē eṉatu vēṇṭukōḷiyum aṉpuṭaṉ ēṟṟu innūlukku aḻakiyatōr aṇinturi aḷittārkaḷ. tamiḻ nalam vaḷarkkum ipperuntakiyārkku eṉatu naṉṟi yiṉiyum vaṇakkattiṉiyum terivittukkoḷkiṉṟēṉ.
oṉṟum pōtāc ciṟiyēṉi ittakiya centamiḻp paṇikku uriyavaṉākkiya vaḷḷal aṇṇāmaliyaracaratu pēraṉpiṉ tiṟatti eḻumiyum niṉintu pōṟṟuṅ kaṭamiyuṭiyēṉ. ippaṇiyiṉi iṉitu niṟivēṟṟi yaruḷiya eṉatu vāḻmutalākiya murukapperumāṉ tiruvaṭikaḷi iṟiñcip pōṟṟukiṉṟēṉ.
innūliṉi aḻakiya muṟiyil ārvamuṭaṉ acciṭṭu taviya aṇṇāmalinakar civakāmi accakattārkku eṉatu pārāṭṭum naṉṟiyum eṉṟum uriyavākum.
tamiḻ māṇavarulakam innūliṉi virumpiyēṟṟup payaṉ koḷḷum eṉa nampukiṟēṉ.
142, kaṉakacapinakar
citamparam	ka.veḷḷivāraṇaṉ
12.09.71






turip palkalikkaḻakat tuṇi vēntar
pērāñiḷīyar ṭākṭar mu.varatarācaṉār, em.ē., piec.ṭi,
avarkaḷ aḷitta
aṇinturi
tolkāppiyam - naṉṉūl ākiya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iraṇṭaṉukku muṉṉum iṭiyilum piṉṉum vēṟu ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa. eṉiṉum, kaṟṟār pōṟṟum ciṟappuṭi yaṉavāy iṉṟuvari viḷaṅkuvaṉa ivviraṇṭumē ākum. ivaṟṟip payilvōr iraṇṭaṉ amippiyum oppu nōkkic cīrtūkka viḻital iyalpu. tolkāppiyattiyum ataṉ uriyiyum oṭṭiyē pavaṇantiyār, naṉṉūli iyaṟṟiṉār eṉpatil aiyamilli. āyiṉum, evvev vakiyil pavaṇantiyār tolkāppiyattiyum uriyiyum pōṟṟiyuḷḷār eṉpatiyum evvevviṭaṅkaḷil māṟṟi yuḷḷār eṉpatiyum uṇartal ārāycciyāḷarkkuk kaṭamiyākiṉṟatu.
aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻttuṟi aṟiñar tiru. ka.veḷḷivāraṇar iru nūlkaḷiyum oppiṭṭu nalla pala viḷakkaṅkaḷiyum, kuṟippukaḷiyum cērttu innūli, tolkāppiyam - naṉṉūl - collatikāram eṉṟa talippil iyaṟṟit tantuḷḷār. mēṟkuṟitta ārāyccikku innūl pērutavi purivatākum. tolkāppiyattil amintuḷḷa muṟippaṭi iyalkaḷiyum, nūṟpākkaḷiyum tantu, avaṟṟiṟku uriya naṉṉūliṉ nūṟpākkaḷi āṅkāṅku amittuḷḷār. tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷukkup paḻiya tamiḻ eṇkaḷiyum, naṉṉūl nūṟpākkaḷukku (avaṟṟiṉ tiripākiya) ulaka eṇkaḷiyu miṭṭu acciṭṭiruppatu kaṟpōrkku utaviyāka uḷḷatu.
aṟiñar tiru.ka.veḷḷivāraṇariṉ centamiḻp pulamiyum ceyvaṉa tiruntac ceyyum māṇpum maṟṟa nūlkaḷil viḷaṅkutal pōlavē, innūlilum pulaṉā kiṉṟaṉa. avartam cīriya muyaṟci ciṟantu mikkōṅkuka.
mu.varatarācaṉ

tolkāppiyam - naṉṉūl/
collatikāram






tolkāppiyam - naṉṉūl
collatikāram
`eḻuttuñ collum poruḷumnāṭi eṉa mēṟ pāyirattuḷ niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē eḻuttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār, ippaṭalattāṟ collilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār. ataṉāl itu collatikāram eṉṉum peyart tāyiṟṟu. col eṉpatu muṟkūṟiya eḻuttiṉāṉ ākkappaṭṭu irutiṇip poruṭṭaṉ miyiyum oruvar uṇarntu koḷḷutaṟkuk karuviyākiya ōciyākum. kiḷavi, col, moḻi eṉpaṉa eḻuttiṉālākiya ōciyiyē kuṟippaṉa. kaṭaloli, caṅkoli, iṭi yoli mutaliyaṉa eḻuttiyal taḻuvā ōcikaḷātaliṉ ivi colleṉap paṭā. ivaṟṟi aravam, oci, ici eṉṟa coṟkaḷāl vaḻaṅkutal marapu. colleṉpatu eḻuttiṉālākkappaṭṭu irutiṇip poruḷ kaḷiyum aṟivikkum ōciyeṉṟum, taṉṉiyuṇara niṉṟavaḻi eḻutteṉavum, piṟa poruḷiyuṇarttiya vaḻic colleṉavum kūṟappaṭumeṉṟum kūṟuvar uriyāciriyar. oruvar tām poruḷi uṇartaṟkum piṟarkku uṇarttaṟkum karuviyāy niṟpatu col. tāṉē oru poruḷik karuti yuṇarttum uṇarvu collukku illi. poruḷiyuṇarttuvā ṉoruvaṉ colliṉ tuṇikoṇṭaṉṟip poruḷi yaṟivuṟutta lākāmiyiṉ avaṉatu toḻilic collākiya karuvimēlēṟṟic col uṇarttumeṉak karuvik karuttāvākak tolkāppiyar.
coṟkaḷip pākupaṭuttu viḷakkak karutiya āciriyar, irutiṇi, aimpāl, eḻuvaki vaḻu, eṭṭu vēṟṟumi, aṟuvakit toki, mūṉṟiṭam, mūṉṟu kālam, iruvaki vaḻakku eṉṉum ivveṭṭu vakiyāṉ ārāyntuṇarttiṉā reṉpar iḷampūraṇar. ivveṭṭiṉōṭu col nāṉku vakiya eṉṟalum, avaṟṟiyē palavākap pakuttalum, vikāravakiyum, poruḷkōḷ vakiyum, ceyyuṭkuriya col nāṉkeṉṟalum, eṉa iviyuṅ kūṭṭi eṭṭiṟanta palavakiyāṉ ārāyntuṇart tiṉāreṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
col taṉimoḻi, toṭar moḻi eṉa iruvakippaṭum. moḻikaḷ yāṅkaṇun taṉittu nillāvēṉum ipporuṭku iccol eṉa aṟivuṭiyōr variyaṟuttuk kūṟiya paṭippuk kālattum, toṭarmoḻic coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟu niṟpa maṟṟiya eñciya vaḻiyum taṉittu niṟṟaluṇṭu. ataṉāl taṉi moḻiyeṉṟa pirivum koḷḷappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. taṉimoḻi, poruṇmi māttiram uṇarttuvatallatu kēṭṭārkkoru payaṉpaṭa niṟpataṉṟu. kēṭṭārkkup poruḷiṉitu viḷakkip payaṉpaṭa niṟpaṉa toṭar moḻikaḷēyām. ākavē toṭar moḻikaḷiṉ iyalpiṉi muṉṉuṇartti avaṟṟukkuk karuviyākiya taṉimoḻi yilakkaṇattiṉip piṉṉuṇarttutalē muṟiyākum. im muṟiyiṉi yuḷattuṭkoṇṭa tolkāppiyar, ippaṭalattuḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya toṭarmoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇatti muṉṉuṇartti, attoṭar moḻikaḷip pakuttukkāṇum muṟiyāl peyarccol, viṉiccol, iṭiccol, uriccol ākiya taṉimoḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷip piṉṉar uṇarttuvar.
coṟkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭaruṅkāl payaṉili vakiyāṉum tokinili vakiyāṉum, eṇṇunili vakiyāṉum toṭarumeṉpatu tamiḻilakkaṇa marapākum. cāttaṉ vantāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla eḻuvāyum payaṉiliyumākat toṭarntu niṟpatu `payaṉilivaki eṉappaṭum. vēṟṟumi yurupum uvama urupum eṇṇummiyākiya iṭiccollum viṉic collīṟum paṇpuṇarttum īṟum iviyallāta piṟitōr collum maṟintu niṟkat toṭarum colliṉatu toṭarcci `toki nilivaki yeṉappaṭum. poruḷkaḷi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērttu eṇṇum muṟiyil aminta coṟkaḷatu toṭarcci `eṇṇunilivaki yeṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu mūvakiyāl toṭarum toṭarmoḻikaḷellāvaṟṟiyum poruḷ nilimi nōkki alvaḻit toṭareṉṟum vēṟṟumit toṭareṉṟum iruvakiyākap pakutturippar tollāciriyar.
poruḷiyiṭamākak koṇṭu nikaḻvatu collākum. collilakkaṇaṅ kūṟakkarutiya āciriyar, accol nikaḻccikku nilikkaḷaṉākiya poruḷkaḷellāvaṟṟiyum uyartiṇi yeṉavum aḵṟiṇiyeṉavum irutiṟaṉāka variyaṟuttu, apporuḷ vakipaṟṟi nikaḻuñ coṟkaḷiyum uyartiṇic colleṉṟum aḵṟiṇic colleṉṟum iruvakiyākap pakutturittār. tiṇiyeṉṉum collukku oḻukkam eṉpatu poruḷākum. makkaḷatu nallaṟiviṉ payaṉāyamivatu oḻukkam. vilaṅku mutaliya ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉiṉṟum pirittu makkaṭ kulattāri uyartiṇiyeṉac ciṟappittu uyarttuvatu, maṉavuṇarviṉ pāṟpaṭṭa nalloḻukkamēyām. ulaka vāḻvil mēṉmēl uyarcciyaṭitaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya ivvoḻukka uṇarvu makkaṭ kulattāriṭamē ciṟappāka amintu vaḷartal karuti avarkaḷi uyartiṇiyeṉat taṉic ciṟappuṭiya tokuti yākavum, naṉṟun tītum pakuttuṇarntoḻukum nallaṟivu vāykkap peṟāta maṟṟiya uyirkaḷiyum uyiral poruḷkaḷiyum oḻukka vuṇarccik kuriyavallāta aḵṟiṇiyeṉac ciṟappil tokutiyākavum muṉṉit tamiḻāciriyar pakuttuḷḷārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam ulakap poruḷḷellāvaṟṟiyum uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi eṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakki avaṟṟi āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aintu pālkaḷākap pakuttu ipporuḷ vēṟupāṭṭiṉi viḷaṅka aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkuriya collamippiṉiyuṭiyatāka nam muṉaṉōr tam tāy moḻiyākiya tamiḻmoḻiyi uruvākki vaḷarttārkaḷ. ivvāṟu coṟkaḷiṉ vāyilākat tiṇi pālkaḷi viḷaṅka aṟivikkum muṟi tamiḻilaṉṟi vēṟem moḻiyiluṅ kāṇappaṭāta ciṟappiyalpākum.
collilakkaṇa vaki
muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa eṇṇiya tolkāppiyaṉār, coṟkaḷip poruḷ nilimi nōkkit toṭarmoḻi, taṉimoḻi yeṉa iruvakippaṭuttu, attoṭarmoḻiyi alvaḻittoṭar, vēṟṟumit toṭar eṉa iruvakip paṭuttu, avviruvakit toṭarum ceppum viṉāvumāka nikaḻtalāl avaṟṟi vaḻuvāmaṟ kūṟutaṟkāka muṟpaṭac coṉṉilimiyāṟ poruḷi uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇiyeṉa iruvakippaṭuttār. avaṟṟuḷ uyartiṇiyuṇarttuñ coṟkaḷi oruvaṉi yaṟiyuñcol, oruttiyi yaṟiyuñcol, palari yaṟiyuñcol eṉa mūvakippaṭuttār. aḵṟiṇi yuṇarttuñ col eṉa iruvakippaṭuttār. iṅṅaṉam irutiṇi aimpāl iyalneṟi vaḻāmal iruvaki vaḻakkilum amikka vēṇṭuñ coṟkaḷi yeṭuttōtiṉār. ataṉpiṉ vēṟṟumit toṭar kūṟuvār, mayaṅkā marapiṉavāki varuvaṉa eḻuvaki vēṟṟumiyuṇarttiṉār; ataṉpiṉ eṭṭāvatākiya viḷi vēṟṟumiyuṇarttiṉār; ataṉpiṉ taṉimoḻip pakutiyākiya peyarccol, viṉic col, iṭiccol, uriccol eṉpavaṟṟiṉ pākupāṭu uṇarttiṉār; ataṉpiṉ coṟkaḷ vikārappaṭumāṟum oṭṭumāṟum eñcumāṟum piṟavum uṇarttiṉār. eḻuttati kārattuḷ eḻuvāy vēṟṟumiyiyum ataṉ tiripākiya viḷivēṟṟumiyiyum alvaḻik kaṇṇē muṭitta āciriyar ivvatikārattē vēṟṟumi kaḷuṭaṉ iyittu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iccollatikāram oṉpatu iyalkaḷāl iyaṉṟatākum. kiḷaviyākkattuḷ alvaḻittoṭarum, vēṟṟumiyiyal, vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal, viḷimarapu eṉṉum mūṉṟiyalkaḷilum vēṟṟumit toṭarum uṇarttappeṟṟaṉa. peyariyalil peyarilakkaṇamum, viṉiyiyalil viṉiyilakkaṇamum, iṭiyiyalil iṭic collilakkaṇamum, uriyiyalil uric collilakkaṇamum, eccaviyaluḷ eñciyaṉa piṟavum uṇarttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvakiyiṉāl ivvatikārattiṉ iyalkaḷum oṉpatāyiṉa.
iṉi, tolkāppiyatti mutaṉūlākak koṇṭu naṉṉūl eṉṉum vaḻinūl ceyta pavaṇanti muṉivar, tām kūṟa eṭuttuk koṇṭa collilakkaṇattiṉip peyariyal, viṉiyiyal, potuviyal, iṭic colliyal, uric colliyal eṉṉum aintiyalkaḷāṟ kūṟukiṉṟār. peyarccoṟkaḷatu iyalpu peyariyalilum, viṉic coṟkaḷatiyalpu viṉiyiyalilum, peyar, viṉi, iṭi, uri, eṉṉum nālvakic coṟkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷ ciṅkanōkkākap potuviyalilum, iṭic coṟkaḷatiyalpu iṭic colliyalilum, uriccoṟkaḷatu iyalpu uriccolliyalilum uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa eṉpar naṉṉūluriyāciriyar mayilinātar.
tolkāppiyac collatikārattil mutaliyalākiya kiḷavi yākkattilum oṉpatām iyalākiya eccaviyalilum kūṟappeṟṟa collilakkaṇaṅkaḷi nālvakic coṟkaḷukkuriya potu vilakkaṇamāta loppumipaṟṟi naṉṉūlāciriyarākiya pavaṇantiyār potuviyal eṉa oriyalāka aṭakki, ataṉip peyariyal viṉiyiyalkaḷukkum  iṭiccol uric colliyal kaḷukkum naṭuvē ciṅka nōkkāka amittuḷḷār. tolkāppiyattuḷ vēṟṟumiyiyal, vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal, viḷimarapu eṉa mūṉṟiyal kaḷāl viritturikkappaṭṭa vēṟṟumi yilakkaṇaṅkaḷip peyarilakkaṇamātal oppumipaṟṟip peyariyalōṭu iṇittup peyariyal eṉa ōriyalākavum, viṉic collilakkaṇattiṉi viṉiyiyal eṉa ōriyalākavum amittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivvakiyāl naṉṉūliṟ collati kāram peyariyal. viṉiyiyal, potuviyal, iṭic colliyal, uric  colliyal eṉṉum aintiyalkaḷiyuṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
 arukatēvaṉi vaḻipaṭum camaṇa camayac cāṉṟōrākiya pavaṇanti muṉivar, tammāl iyaṟṟappeṟum nūl iṉitu niṟi vēṟutal vēṇṭit teyva vaṇakkamum ceyappaṭu poruḷum eytavurikkum taṟciṟappup pāyiramāka ivvati kārattiṉ toṭakkattiṟ kūṟiyatu,
		muccaka niḻaṟṟu muḻumati mukkuṭi
accuta ṉaṭitoḻu taṟikuvaṉ collē.
eṉṉum nūṟpāvākum. itu kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkamum atikāramum uṇarttuvatu.
itaṉ poruḷ :- mūṉṟulakattilumuḷḷa uyirkaṭ kellām niḻalic ceyyum niṟinta matiyip pōlum mūṉṟu kuṭiyiyuṭiya aḻivillātavaṉuṭiya aṭikaḷi vaṇaṅkic collilakkaṇatti yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ, eṉṟavāṟu. ellāc camayattōrāṉum vaṇaṅkappaṭum paṭittal, kāttal, aḻittal ākiya toḻilkaḷukkuriya ellāk kaṭavuḷākiyum niṉṟāṉ oruvaṉē eṉṉum potu nōkkuṭaṉ arukatēvaṉi vaṇaṅkum pavaṇanti muṉivar ammutalvaṉi eḻuttatikārattil
		pūmali yacōkiṉ puṉiniḻa lamarnta
nāṉmukaṟ ṟoḻutunaṉ kiyampuva ṉeḻuttē.
eṉap paṭittaṟṟoḻil paṟṟi nāṉmukaṉ eṉap pōṟṟiyatu pōṉṟu kāttaṟṟoḻil paṟṟi ivvatikārattil accutaṉ eṉṟa peyarāṟ pōṟṟiyuḷḷami oppunōkki yuṇarat takuvatākum.

1. kiḷaviyākkam
kiḷavi - col. ākkam - ātal. coṟkaḷ poruḷ mēl āmāṟuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ kiḷaviyākka meṉṉum peyart tāyiṟṟu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, vaḻukkaḷintu coṟkaḷi amittuk koṇṭamiyāl kiḷaviyākkamāyiṟṟu eṉac cēṉāvariyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, coṟkaḷ oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu toṭarntu poruḷmēl ākum nilimiyik kūṟuvatu ivviyalātaliṉ kiḷaviyākkam eṉṉum peyart tāyiṟṟu eṉat teyvac ciliyārum ivviyalukkup peyark kāraṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. kiḷaviyatu ākkattik kūṟuvatu kiḷaviyākkam eṉa vēṟṟumit tokippuṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokiyākak koṇṭār teyvacciliyār. ivviyaliṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ  aṟupattiraṇṭeṉa iḷampūraṇar nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum, aṟupattoṉṟeṉac cēṉāvariyarum aimpat toṉpateṉat teyvac ciliyārum pakuttu uri kūṟi yuḷḷārkaḷ.
	1.	uyartiṇi meṉmaṉār makkaṭ cuṭṭē
aḵṟiṇi yeṉmaṉār avarala piṟavē
āyiru tiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉa collē.
itu, colliyum poruḷiyum variyaṟutturikkiṉṟatu.	
itaṉ poruḷ :- uyartiṇiyeṉṟu colluvar, makkaḷeṉṟu cuṭṭappaṭum poruḷi; aḵṟiṇiyeṉṟu colluvar avarallāta (uyirp) poruḷiyum piṟa (uyiral) poruḷiyum; avvirutiṇip poruḷkaḷiyum coṟkaḷ uṇarttum eṉṟavāṟu.
uyartiṇiyum  aḵṟiṇiyum eṉap poruḷkaḷ iru tiṟappaṭutalāl, avaṟṟi uṇarttum coṟkaḷum uyar tiṇiccol aḵṟiṇic col eṉa iruvakippaṭum eṉa variyaṟuttavāṟu.
uyartiṇi - uyarnta oḻukkam; aḵtu, ākupeyarāy, uyartaṟkēṟṟa oḻukkamuṭiya makkaḷiṉatti yuṇartti niṉṟatu. aimpulavuṇarvākiya aiyaṟivuṭiya mākkaḷiṉ vēṟupaṭṭut tītorīi naṉṟiṉpāṟ celuttum maṉavuṇarvākiya āṟāvataṟi viṉip peṟṟu `makkaḷ tāmē yāṟaṟivuyirē (tol - marapu - 33.) eṉa uyarttuk kūṟum naṉku matippup paṟṟi `uyartiṇi eṉmaṉār makkaṭcuṭṭē eṉṟār. uyartiṇi eṉpatu viṉittoki. uyarnta makkaḷ, uyarāniṉṟa makkaḷ, uyarum makkaḷ eṉa muk kālamum viritturittaṟkēṟṟa vakiyil ittoṭar amintuḷḷami kāṇka. eṉmaṉār  eṉpatu ār īṟṟu etirkāla viṉimuṟṟu makkaṭ caṭṭu - makkaḷākiya naṉku matikkappaṭum poruḷ. cuṭṭu - naṉku matippu; aḵtu ākapeyarāy naṉku matikkappaṭum poruḷi uṇartti niṉṟatu. makkaṭ cuṭṭu eṉṟa toṭaril muṉṉiṉṟa makkaḷ eṉpatu iyalpeyarp poruḷi yuṇarttap piṉṉiṉṟa cuṭṭu eṉṉum peyarē ākupeyarp poruḷi yuṇartta irupeyarum oruporuḷiyē uṇarttum muṟiyil oṭṭi niṉṟaṉavātalāl itaṉi iru peyaroṭṭāku peyareṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
makkaḷāvār orutaṉmiyaraṉṟi āṇ, peṇ, ali eṉṉum vaṭivuvēṟṟumi uṭiyarākaliṉ, avarellāṭarittum potuvāka amintuḷḷa makkaṭṭaṉmiyik kuṟittu makkaḷ ivar eṉṉum potup poruṇmi uyartiṇiyām eṉpataṟi vittaṟku makkaḷ eṉṉātu makkaṭ cuṭṭu eṉṟār eṉavum, makkaḷallāta uyiruṭiyaṉavum uyirillaṉavākiya piṟavum aḵṟiṇiyām eṉpataṟivittaṟku avaralapiṟa eṉṟār eṉavum kūṟuvar teyvac ciliyār. uyartiṇiyallāta tiṇi aḵṟiṇiyātaliṉ altiṇi aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟāyiṟṟu. uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉpaṉa tam kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa tollāciriyar vaḻaṅkiya ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭṭuc coṟ kaḷātaliṉ uyartiṇi makkaḷ, aḵṟiṇi avarallātaṉavum piṟavum eṉa accoṟkaḷukkup poruḷ viḷakkam tantār tolkāppiyar. av + irutiṇi = āyirutiṇi yeṉac cuṭṭu nīṇṭatu. āyiru tiṇiyiṉ eṉpuḻic cāriyiyuḷvaḻi iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi yurupu maṟintu niṉṟatu. ivvāṟē `maṟaṅkaṭinta varuṅkaṟpiṉ eṉavum `cila colliṟ palkūntal eṉavum piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷakattum iṉcāriyi urupu paṟṟātu niṟṟal kāṇka. icittal - olittal; īṇṭu iccol uṇarttal eṉṉum poruḷil āḷappeṟṟatu. `avaralapiṟa eṉpuḻi avaralavum piṟavum eṉa ummi viritturippar teyvacciliyār. `avarala piṟa eṉpuḻi avariṉ allavākiya piṟa eṉa nīkkap poruḷtarum iṉṉurupu viritturittalum uṇṭu.
collatikārat toṭakkattil colliṉ kūṟupāṭum ataṉ iyalpum uṇarttap pōnta naṉṉūlāciriyar, 
	259.	orumoḻi toṭarmoḻi potumoḻi yeṉṟā
irutiṇi yimpāṟ poruḷiyun taṉṉiyum
mūvaki yiṭattum vaḻakkoṭu ceyyuḷiṉ
veḷippaṭi kuṟippiṉ virippatu collē.
eṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) oru moḻiyum toṭarmoḻiyum potu moḻiyum eṉa mūṉṟu kūṟupāṭṭiṉiyuṭiyatāy, irutiṇi yākiya aimpāṟ poruḷiṉiyum apporuḷi yaṉṟit taṉṉiyum taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkki eṉṉum mūṉṟiṭat tiṉum ulaka vaḻakkilum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum veḷippaṭiyālum kuṟippiṉālum viḷakkuvatu collām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, ellāc collum orumoḻi toṭarmoḻi potumoḻi eṉṉum mūṉṟāyum, ellāpporuḷum irutiṇi aimpālāyum aṭaṅkumeṉpatum, uyirkku aṟivu karuviyāy niṉṟu taṉṉiyum poruḷiyum uṇarttumāṟu pōla, oruvarkkuc colkaruviyāy niṉṟu taṉṉiyum irutiṇi yimpāṟ poruḷiyum uṇarttumeṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
mēlic cūttirattu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē mūvaki moḻikaḷ āmāṟu uṇarttap pōnta pavaṇantiyār,
	260.	orumoḻi yoruporu ḷaṉavān toṭarmoḻi
palaporu ḷaṉapotu virumiyu mēṟpaṉa.
eṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) pakāppatamēṉum pakupatamēṉum oṉṟu niṉṟu tattam oruporuḷit taruvaṉa orumoḻikaḷeṉappaṭum. avviru vakip pataṅkaḷum taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum alvaḻi vēṟṟumip poruḷ nōkkattāl iraṇṭu mutaliyaṉavākat toṭarntu niṉṟu iraṇṭu mutaliya palaporuḷit taruvaṉa toṭarmoḻikaḷeṉappaṭum. oṉṟāyniṉṟu oru poruḷ tantum atuvē toṭarntu niṉṟu palaporuḷ tantum iv viraṇṭaṟkum potuvāy niṟpaṉa potumoḻika ḷeṉappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) nampi, naṅki, māntar, cāttaṉ, cātti, nilam, nīr, maṇ, poṉ, vantāṉ, vantāḷ, vantār, vantatu, vantaṉa, til, maṉ, cāla, tava eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa oru moḻi. nilam valitu, atukol, cālappaki, nilaṅ kaṭantāṉ, nilattik kaṭantāṉ, nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumāl eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa toṭarmoḻi. eṭṭu, koṭṭu, tāmari, vēṅki, eḻuntiruntāṉ, vārā niṉṟāṉ, urittiṭ ṭāṉ eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattaṉa potumoḻi. ivi oru moḻi kaḷāy oruporuḷ taruvataṉṟi, eḷḷittu, koḷḷittu, tāvukiṉṟa mari, vēkiṉṟa ki, eḻuntu piṉ iruntāṉ, vantu niṉṟāṉ, urittup piṉ iṭṭāṉ eṉat toṭarmoḻikaḷāyp palaporuḷ tarutalum kāṇka. īṇṭut toṭarmoḻi palaporuḷaṉa eṉṟatu pala colliṟ pala poruḷiyeṉa aṟika. cēṉi, paṭi mutaliya oru moḻikaḷ yāṉi tēr kutiri, kālāḷ ākiya pala poruḷi yuṇarttu mēṉum palaporuḷīṭṭamākiya aviyaṉittum iyintu oṉṟāyuḷḷa niliyil oruporuḷeṉavē karutappaṭum eṉpatu,
		`palavi ṉiyintavum oṉṟeṉap paṭumē
aṭicil pottakañ cēṉi yaminta
katava māli kampala maṉiya.                 (akattiyam)
eṉavarum paḻañ cūttirattālaṟiyappaṭum.
irutiṇiyāmāṟituveṉa viḷakkap pōnta pavaṇantiyār, `uyartiṇiyeṉmaṉār makkaṭcuṭṭē eṉavarum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvi yaṭiyoṟṟi,
	261.	makka ṭēvar naraka ruyartiṇi
maṟṟuyi ruḷḷavu millavu maḵṟiṇi.
eṉac cūttirañ ceytār.
(i-ḷ) makkaḷum tēvarum narakarum uyartiṇiyām; avarallāta (māvum puḷḷum mutaliya) uyiruḷḷaṉavum (nilam, nīr, vaḷi mutaliya) uyirillaṉavum aḵṟiṇiyām eṉṟavāṟu.
makkaḷ, tēvar, narakar ivvāṟu iṉamuṟiyil vittup pēcappaṭutal avvavviṉattukkuriya uṭampuṭaṉ uyir kūṭiniṉṟavaḻiyē yātaliṉ, avvuṭampum uyirum vēṟākap pirinta niliyilum avaṟṟi vēṟupaṭavittu eṇṇiya niliyilum avi aḵṟiṇiyākavē koḷḷappaṭum. makkaḷākap piṟantu nalviṉi tīviṉikaḷic ceytu nalviṉi yākiya puṇṇiyattiṉ mikutiyāl tēvarāyum, tīviṉiyākiya pāvattiṉ mikutiyāl narakarāyum piṟattaliṉ, makkaḷ tēvar narakar eṉac cātaṉa cāttiya muṟiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. makkaḷeṉṟu karutappaṭum poruḷē uyartiṇi, avarallāta piṟavellām aḵṟiṇi eṉa variyaṟuttukkūṟiya tolkāppiyar, piṉṉar `ivveṉa aṟiyum antam tamakkila vākiya teyvañcuṭṭiya peyar mutaliyaṉavum `uyartiṇi maruṅkiṟ pāl pirinticikkum eṉak kūṟutalālum `makkaḷ tāmēyāṟaṟivuyirē (marapiyal - 33) eṉa variyaṟuttōtip piṉ makkaḷiṉattōṭu toṭarpuṭiya kiḷiyeṉap paṭuvārāy maṉavuṇarvuṭiya āṟaṟivuyiruḷ vittuk karututaṟkuriya tēvar tāṉavar mutaliyōriyum ōrāṟṟāṉ uyartiṇiyuṭ cērttu eṇṇutaṟkuriya muṟiyil `piṟavu muḷavē yakkiḷip piṟappē eṉa mīṇṭum eṭuttōti mutaliyōriyum uyartiṇivakiyuṭ cārttik kūṟum piṟkāla vaḻakkiṟku āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār iṭantantuḷḷami iṅku niṉittaṟkuriyatākum.
maram nāṭoṟum uyarntami karuti uyarmaram eṉa vaḻaṅkumāṟu pōṉṟum kalviyaṟivu nāṭoṟum uyarntami karuti ataṉiyuṭiyār mēlēṟṟi uyarmakkaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkumāṟu pōṉṟum oḻukkattāl nāḷum uyarum puṭi peyarcci karuti avvuyarcciyuṭiya makkaṭṭokutiyi uyartiṇi eṉak kūṟiṉārātaliṉ, uyartiṇi eṉpatu viṉittoki eṉa iḷampūraṇar mutaliya paṇṭiyuriyāciriyar kūṟiyatu mikavum poruttamuṭiyatātal kāṇalām.
	2.	āṭūu vaṟicol makaṭūu vaṟicol
pallō raṟiyuñ collōṭu civaṇi
ammup pāṟcol uyartiṇi yavvē.
iḵtu uyartiṇiyuṭ pālvakiyiṉi yuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) āṇmakaṉiyaṟiyum collum peṇmakaḷi yaṟiyuñ collum pallōriyuñ colloṭu porunti ammūṉṟu kūṟṟuc collum uyartiṇiyiṭattaṉavām. eṉṟavāṟu.
āṭūu - āṇmakaṉ. makaṭūu - peṇmakaḷ. āṭavar eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu āṇpaṉmiyum peṇṭir eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup peṇpaṉmiyum makkaḷ eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattu iruvar paṉmiyum aṭaṅkap pallōraṟiyuñ col eṉṟār. aṟicol - aṟitaṟkuk karuviyākiya col. civaṇutal - poruntutal. civaṇi eṉṉum ceyteṉ eccam uyartiṇiya eṉṉum viṉikkuṟippuk koṇṭu muṭintatu.
uyartiṇic coṟkaḷ muppālkaḷākap pakutturikkap paṭum eṉpataṉi,
	262.	āṇpeṇ palareṉa muppāṟ ṟuyartiṇi.
eṉavarum cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār naṉṉūlār.
	3.	oṉṟaṟi collē palavaṟi colleṉ
ṟāyiru pāṟco laḵṟiṇi yavvē.
iḵtu aḵṟiṇiyuṭ pālvakiyiṉi uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉṟaṉiyaṟiyuñ collum palavaṟṟi yaṟiyuñ collum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu kūṟṟuc collum aḵṟiṇiyiṭattaṉavām eṉṟavāṟu.
aḵṟiṇic coṟkaḷ oṉṟu, pala eṉṉum irupāl kaḷākap pakutturikkappaṭuvaṉa eṉpataṉi,
	263.	oṉṟē palaveṉ ṟirupāṟ ṟaḵṟiṇi.
eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟittār naṉṉūlār. aḵṟiṇik kaṇṇum cēval, kaḷiṟu mutaliya āṇpālum peṭi piṭi mutaliya peṇpālum uḷavāyiṉum, avvāṇmi peṇmiyākiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ uyiruḷḷaṉavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟukkum uyirillaṉa vaṟṟukkum iṉmiyāṉ appakuppoḻittu ellāvaṟṟiṟkum porunta oṉṟaṉpālākak kūṟiṉār.
	4.	peṇmi cuṭṭiya vuyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ
āṇmi tirinta peyarnilik kiḷaviyun
teyvañ cuṭṭiya peyarnilik kiḷaviyum
ivveṉa vaṟiyu mantantamak kilavē
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṟ pālpirin ticikkum.
itu, pāluḷaṭaṅkāta pēṭiyiyum tiṇiyaḷaṭaṅkāta teyvattiyum pāluḷḷun tiṇiyuḷḷum aṭakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) uyartiṇiyiṭattup peṇmit taṉmikuṟitta āṇmi tirinta peyarccollum, teyvattik kuṟitta peyarccollum, iviyeṉat tam poruḷi vēṟaṟiya niṟkum īṟṟeḻuttiṉit tamakkuṭiyavalla; uyartiṇi yiṭattiṟ kuriya pālāy vēṟupaṭṭicippaṉa. eṉṟavāṟu.
pāl vēṟupaṭṭicittalāvatu, tām uyartiṇip peyarāy āṭūuvaṟicol mutalāyiṉavaṟṟukkuriya īṟṟeḻuttē tam viṉikku īṟāka icittal.
āṭavar makaḷir eṉṉum irutiṟattāruḷ tattamakku iyalpāka amiya vēṇṭiya taṉmiyil māṟupaṭṭup peṇ taṉmi mikku āṇtaṉmi kuṟintārip pēṭi eṉavum, āṇumiṉṟip peṇṇumiṉṟi irutaṉmiyum viraviya niliyiluḷḷāri ali eṉavum vaḻaṅkutal toṉṟutoṭṭu varum tamiḻ marapu. avaṟṟuḷ `uyartiṇi maruṅkiṟ peṇmi cuṭṭiya āṇmi tirinta peyarnilik kiḷavi eṉṟatu pēṭiyi peṇmi cuṭṭiya eṉṉum peyareccam peyarnilik kiḷavi eṉṉum peyar koṇṭatu. āṇmi tirinta eṉpatu peyar nilikkiḷavikku aṭimoḻiyāy iṭi niṉṟatu. āṇmiyum peṇmiyum allāta aliyiṉit `taṉmi tiripeyar eṉap (tol-col-kiḷavi 56) piṉṉark kuṟippiṭuvar. alippeyariṉ nīkkutaṟkup peṇmi cuṭṭiya eṉṟum, makaṭūup peyariṉ nīkkutaṟku āṇmi tirinta eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. peṇmi cuṭṭiya eṉavē peṇmi cuṭṭāta niliyiṟ pēṭu eṉṉum colli vaḻaṅkutal tolkāppiyar kālattu ilakkaṇa marapaṉṟu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. peṇmi tiritalum uṇṭēṉum āṇmi tirital perumpāṉmiyākalāṉ āṇmi tirinta peyarnilik kiḷaviyi iṅku eṭuttōtiṉār. peṇmi tirintataṉi `makaṭūu maruṅkiṟ pāltiri kiḷavi (tol - peyariyal - 194) eṉap piṉṉar eṭuttōtuvar.
pēṭiyar, pēṭimār, pēṭikaḷ eṉpaṉavum aṭaṅkutaṟkup `pēṭiyeṉṉum peyarnilik kiḷavi eṉa vitantu kūṟātu, `peṇmi cuṭṭiya āṇmi tirinta peyarnilik kiḷavi eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉār. `peṇṇavāy āṇiḻanta pēṭi yaṇiyāḷō, kaṇṇavāttakka kalam eṉavarum paḻam pāṭalāl peṇtaṉmi mēṟpaṭṭu āṇ taṉmi kuṟintataṉiyē pēṭi eṉap peṇpāṟ paṭuttu vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkam iṉitu pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
(u-m) pēṭi vantāḷ, pēṭiyar, vantār eṉavum, vācutēvaṉ vantāṉ, tiruviṉāḷ vantāḷ, muppattu mūvarum vantār eṉavum varum.
antam eṉṟatu īṟṟeḻuttākiya vikutiyiṉi. antam  tamakkilaḷē eṉṉum ilēciṉāl narakaṉ vantāṉ, naraki vantāḷ, narakar vantār eṉpaṉa koḷka eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
makkaṭ piṟappilē tōṉṟip peṇ taṉmi mikuntum āṇ taṉmi kuṟintum āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru taṉmikaḷum viraviyuḷḷa pēṭṭiṉik kuṟitta peyarccollum teyvattik kuṟitta peyarccollum irutiṇi aimpālkaḷuḷ iṉṉapāl eṉat teritaṟkuk karuviyākiya vikutiyiṉi uṭiyaṉa vallavā taliṉ avi uyartiṇip peyarāy niṉṟu āṇpāṟ col mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vikutiyiṉiyē tam viṉikku īṟākap peṟṟu iṉṉapāl eṉa viḷaṅki niṟpaṉa eṉpataṉi ic cūttirattāl tolkāppiyar teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷami kāṇalām.
innūlpāviṟ kūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ teyvañ cuṭṭiya peyari `makkaḷ tēvar narakar uyartiṇi eṉa uyartiṇi yuṭpaṭuttik kūṟiya naṉṉūlār, iccūttirattiṟ kuṟitta peṇmi cuṭṭiya uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ āṇmi tirinta peyarākiya pēṭiyiṉiyum taṉmitiripeyar eṉavum makaṭūu maruṅkiṟ pāltirikiḷavi yeṉavum piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum ali, makaṇmā mutaliyavaṟṟiyum kuṟikkum muṟiyiṟ `pēṭu eṉṉum potuc collip payaṉpaṭutti avaṟṟukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟi avaṟṟip pāluḷḷum tiṇiyuḷḷum aṭakkukiṉṟār. avvāṟu aṭakkuvatāka amintatu,
	264.	peṇmiviṭ ṭāṇavā vuvapē ṭāṇpāl
āṇmiviṭ ṭalla tavāvuva peṇpāl
irumiyum aḵṟiṇi yaṉṉavu mākum.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
(i-ḷ) peṇ taṉmiyiṉi viṭṭu āṇ taṉmiyiṉi avāvuvaṉavākiya pēṭukaḷ uyartiṇi yāṇpālām. āṇ taṉmiyiṉi viṭṭup peṇ taṉmiyiṉi avāvuvaṉavākiya pēṭukaḷ uyartiṇip peṇpālām; ivviruvakip pēṭukaḷum uyartiṇiyā (yp pāl pirinticit) talēyaṉṟi aḵṟiṇiyi oppaṉavumām. eṉṟavāṟu.
īṇṭu viṭutal eṉṉum col kuṟitaliyum avāvutal eṉṉum col mikutaliyum uṇartti niṉṟaṉa. āṇi lakkaṇamum peṇṇilakkaṇamum viravi oruvakiyēṉum  nirampātu niṟkum pēṭṭiṉip pāṟpotumiyiṉ nīkkik kuṟintatiṉiṉṟum pirittu mikkataṉōṭum cērkkum muṟiyiṉi aṟivuṟuttuvār, peṇmiyiṟ kuṟintu āṇmiyiṉ mikkaṉavākiya pēṭu āṇpāl eṉavum, āṇmiyiṟ kuṟintu peṇmiyiṉ mikkaṉa vākiya pēṭu peṇpāl eṉavum pakutturittār. irutiṟamum viravi niṟkum ippēṭukaḷi attaṉmikaḷiṉ mikuti kuṟivu nōkkātu potuppaṭa nōkkiya vaḻi āṇ peṇ eṉṉum oṉṟaṉpāṟpaṭuttal kūṭāmiyiṉ appāliṉavām uyartiṉiyu miṉṟi, aḵṟiṇi yilakkaṇa miṉmiyiṉ aḵṟiṇiyumaṉṟi, uyiruḷḷaṉavaṟṟuḷ āṇ peṇ eṉṉum pakuppilavāki niṉṟa aḵṟiṇiyi yokkum eṉpār `irumiyum aḵṟiṇi yaṉṉavumākum eṉṟār. iṅṅaṉam potuppaṭak kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ peṇmiviṭṭu āṇ avāvuvaṉavākiya pēṭṭiṉi makaṭūu maruṅkiṟ pāltiri kiḷavi eṉavum, āṇmi viṭṭu allatu avāvuvaṉavākiya pēṭṭiṉi `peṇmi cuṭṭiya - āṇmi tirinta - peyarnilik kiḷavi, eṉavum āṇmi peṇmiyākiya ivvirutaṉmiyum allāta aliyiṉit `taṉmi tiripeyar eṉavum tolkāppiyar vēṟupirittuk kūṟiyuḷḷami iṅku niṉikkattakuvatākum.
āṇuṟuppiṟ kuṟiviṉṟi āṇṭaṉmi yiḻantatūum peṇṇuṟuppiṟ kuṟiviṉṟip peṇ taṉmi yiḻantatūum, peṇpiṟappiṟ ṟōṉṟip peṇṇuṟuppiṉṟit tāṭi tōṟṟi āṇ pōlat tirivatūum eṉa ali mūvakippaṭum, eṉpar teyvac ciliyār. accattiṉ āṇmiyiṟ ṟirintārip pēṭi eṉap peṇṇiyalpu mikkārāka iḻittuk kūṟum vaḻakkum īṇṭuk karutaṟ kuriyatākum.
	5.	ṉaḵkāṉoṟṟē yāṭūu vaṟicol.
iḵtu āṭūu vaṟicol āmāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ṉakara voṟṟiṉi yīṟākavuṭiya col āṇ makaṉi yaṟiyuñ collām. eṉṟavāṟu.
āṭūu - āṇmakaṉ. āṇpāl uṇarttum aṉ āṉ eṉṉum iruvikutikaḷiyum tokuttu ṉaḵkāṉoṟṟu eṉṟār.
(u-m) uṇṭaṉaṉ, uṇṭāṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟaṉaṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟāṉ, uṇpaṉ, uṇpāṉ eṉavum kariyaṉ, kariyāṉ eṉavum varum.
iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷil iṟutik kaṇṇatākiya ṉakara moḻinta piṟaveḻuttukkaḷ ēṉippāṟkum varutaliṉ pāluṇarttutaṟkaṇ ṉakarattiṉ ciṟappu nōkki ṉaḵkā ṉoṟṟē eṉṟār. ēkāram acinili. `ṉaḵkāṉoṟṟu āṭuvaṟi col eṉṟārāyiṉum āṭūuvaṟi collāvatu ṉaḵkāṉoṟṟu eṉpatu karuttākak koḷka. ḷaḵkāṉoṟṟu mutalākap piṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum iviyokkum. iccūttiratti aṭiyoṟṟiyamintatu,
	324.	aṉṉā ṉiṟumoḻi yāṇpāṟ paṭarkki.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
	6.	ḷaḵkāṉoṟṟē makaṭūu vaṟicol.
itu, makaṭūu vaṟicol āmāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ḷaḵkāṉākiya oṟṟiṉi yīṟāka vuṭiya col peṇmakaḷiyaṟiyum collām. eṉṟavāṟu.
makaṭūu - peṇmakaḷ.

	11.	viṉiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum pālaṟi kiḷaviyum

	12.	āṇmi tirinta peyarnilik kiḷavi

(u-m) `māvīḻ nocci eṉpataṟkup poruḷ yātu? eṉa viṉāviya vaḻi `vaṇṭuvīḻntu tēṉuṇṇum noccippū eṉa ataṉic cuṭṭik kūṟutal cuṭṭu eṉappaṭum. `cāttā itu ceyvāyō eṉṟu viṉāyavaḻi `ceyyēṉ eṉa maṟuttal maṟi; `ceyvēṉ eṉṟu uṭampaṭutal nērtal; `nī cey eṉpatu ēval; `ceyvēṉō eṉṟal etirviṉātal; uṭampu nontatu eṉṟal, uṟṟaturittal; uṭampunōm eṉṟal uṟuvatu kūṟal; `maṟṟiyatu ceyvēṉ eṉṟal iṉamoḻi. `kaṅkiyāṭip pōntēṉ cōṟutammiṉ eṉa viṉāviṉṟiyum viṭinikaḻtal uṇṭu. iḵtu, oṉṟaṉi aṟiyaluṟutali yuṇarttātu oṉṟaṉi aṟivuṟuttu niṟṟaliṟ ceppiṉpāṟpaṭum eṉpar cēṉāvariyar.

		`aṟiyāṉ viṉātal aṟivoppuk kāṇṭal

cēṉāvariyar mūvakiyākat tokuttuk kūṟiya aṟiporuḷ viṉā, aṟiyāṉ viṉā, aiyaviṉā eṉṉum im mūṉṟuṭaṉ koḷal viṉā, koṭi viṉā, ēval viṉā eṉṉum mūṉṟiyum kūṭṭi aṟuvaki viṉāvākak koṇṭār pavaṇanti muṉivar.

	267.	ilakkaṇa muṭiya tilakkaṇap pōli
marūu veṉṟākum mūvaki yiyalpum
iṭakka raṭakkal maṅkalaṅ kuḻūukkuṟi
eṉumut takutiyō ṭāṟām vaḻakkiyal.

(u-m) nilam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, ākāyam, makkaḷ, makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉa ilakkaṇamuṭiyaṉa. ilmuṉ eṉpataṉi muṉṟileṉṟum kaṇmī eṉpataṉi mīkaṇ eṉṟum yāvar eṉpataṉi yār eṉṟum ippeṟṟiyāṉ ilakkaṇamuṭiyaṉa pōṉṟu varuvaṉa ilakkaṇap pōli. arumaruntaṉṉāṉ eṉpataṉi arumantāṉ eṉṟum maliyamāṉāṭu eṉpataṉi malāṭu eṉṟam ivvāṟu citintu maruvi varuvaṉa marūuvākum. immūṉṟum oru kāraṇamiṉṟiyē iyalpāy varutaliṉ iyalpu vaḻakku eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iṭakkar - aviyal kiḷavi; aṭakkal ataṉi maṟittuk kūṟutal. `puli niṉṟiṟanta nīral līrattu, `karumuka manti `karumuka manti `cempiṉēṟṟi eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa iṭakkaraṭakkal. ōliyit tirumukameṉṟum kārāṭṭi veḷḷāṭeṉṟum kūṟuvaṉa maṅkala marapiṉāṉ varuvaṉa. yāṉip pākar āṭiyik kāriyeṉṟum vēṭar kaḷḷic colviḷampi yeṉṟum ivvāṟu oru kuḻuviṉar tamakkup pulaṉāka iṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa kuḻūuk kuṟiyeṉappaṭum. ivi mūṉṟum uyarntōrum iḻintōrum `ivvāṟu moḻivatu takkatu eṉṟu koṇṭu vaḻaṅkutaliṉ `takuti vaḻakku eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
	18.	iṉaccuṭṭillāp paṇpukoḷ peyarkkoṭi

itu, ceyyuṭku āvatōr muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) iṉapporuḷic cuṭṭutalaṉṟip paṇpaṭuttu vaḻaṅkappaṭum peyari (oru poruḷukku)k koṭuttu vaḻaṅkutal vaḻakku neṟiyalla, ceyyuḷ neṟiyākum.
tāmari, āmpal eṉpaṉa cemmiyum veṇmiyu muṭiya pūkkaḷik kuṟikkum muṟiyiṟ potuvāy niṉṟa niliyiṟ centāmari, cētāmpal eṉa avaṟṟiṉkaṇ aminta paṇpiṉi aṭimoḻiyāka iyittuk kūṟum niliyiṟ cemmi eṉṉum av aṭimoḻi ataṉiṉ vēṟāka veṇmiyuṭiya veṇṭāmari mutaliya iṉamuṇṭu eṉpataṉic cuṭṭi niṟṟalāl avi iṉaccuṭṭuṭiya paṇpu koḷ peyarāyiṉ. aṅṅaṉam pala poruṭkup potuvākātu ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉa ovvoṉṟaṟkē yuriyavāy vaḻaṅkum peyaroṭu apporuḷiṉ kaṇ aminta cemmi, veṇmi, vemmiyākiya paṇpiṉi aṭimoḻiyākak kūṭṭic ceññāyiṟu, veṇṭiṅkaḷ, vevvaḻal eṉa vaḻaṅkutal muṟkuṟitta ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tīyiṉ vēṟākak karuñāyiṟum karuntiṅkaḷum taṇṇaḻalum uḷavākac cuṭṭāmiyiṉ avi iṉaccuṭṭillāp paṇpukoḷ peyarāyiṉa. iṅṅaṉam iṉañ cuṭṭāta paṇpiṉi aṭi moḻiyākap peṟṟa peyarkaḷ ceyyuḷ neṟiyilaṉṟi vaḻakku neṟiyil vaḻaṅkap peṟutal illi eṉpatu innūṟpāvil aṟivuṟuttappaṭum vitiyākum.
(u-m) 
		` ceññāyiṟṟu nilavu vēṇṭiṉum
 veṇṭiṅkaḷuḷ veyil vēṇṭiṉum
(puṟam 38 : 7 - 8)
eṉa iṉac cuṭṭillāp paṇpu koḷ peyar ceyyuḷil vaḻaṅkap peṟutal kāṇalām. paṇpiṉi aṭimoḻiyākak koḷḷātu, vaṭavēṅkaṭam - teṉkumari, muṭṭāḻi, kōṭcuṟā eṉatticiyum uṟuppum toḻilumutalāya aṭiyaṭuttu iṉañcuṭṭātu varuvaṉa oṉṟeṉamuṭitta leṉpataṉāṟ ceyyuḷāṟeṉa amittuk koḷḷappaṭum eṉavum, `paṇpu koḷ peyar iṉaṅkuṟittu varutal marapu; ammarapu vaḻakkiṉ kaṇ vaḻuvaṟka eṉṟum ceyyuṭkaṇ vaḻuvamikkaveṉṟum kāttavāṟāyiṟṟu eṉavum iccūttirap poruḷi viḷakkuvar cēṉāvariyar.
iṉi, oruvaṉ colluvatu; `vaḻakkāṟalla eṉpataṉi `vaḻakkāṟṟiṉ alla eṉa aintāmurupu virittu, `alla eṉpataṉip peyarppaṭuttuk kūṟum; kūṟavē, vaḻakkiṟkum iṉamil paṇpukoḷ peyarkaḷ uḷa eṉpatu pōntatām eṉap piṟar kūṟum ōr uriyiṉik kuṟittu, peruṅ koṟṟaṉ, peruṅkūttaṉ eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa, vaḻakkiṭip paṇpup peyar varuṅkāl kuṇamiṉṟi viḻumitākac collutaṟku vantu niṟkum eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭut tantu  viḷakkuvar iḷampūraṇar. `vaḻakkāṟṟiṉ alla eṉṟa toṭar ceyyuḷāṟeṉap : paṭṭa paṇpukoḷ peyarkaḷ vaḻakkāṟṟiṉ vārātaṉa, alla, varuvaṉavē eṉṟa poruḷil iṅku āḷap peṟṟiruttaliyuṅ koṇṭa pavaṇanti muṉivar,
	400.	poruṇ mutalāṟām aṭi cēr moḻi, iṉam
uḷḷavum illavumām iru vaḻakkiṉum.
eṉac cūttirañ ceytār. poruḷāti āṟaṉiyum aṭiyāka aṭuttu varumoḻikaḷ iṉattik kāṭṭuvaṉavum kāṭṭātaṉavu mām vaḻakkiṭattum ceyyuḷiṭattum eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
(u-m) neykkuṭam, kuḷanel, kārttikiviḷakku, pūntōṭu, centāmari, kuṟuṅkūli eṉap poruḷātiyāṟum ākiya aṭimoḻikaḷ vaḻakkiṭattu iṉamuṇarttiṉa; uppaḷam, ūrmaṉṟu, kīḻnōkkiya kiṇaṟu, mēlnōkkiya maram, ciṟukāli, kāṉmāṭu, cempōttu, tōytayir eṉa vaḻakkiṭattu iṉamiṉṟi vantaṉa. ivvāṟē ivvaṭi moḻikaḷ ceyyuḷil iṉa muḷḷaṉavum illaṉavumāka varutali utāraṇaṅkāṭṭi viḷakkiya mayilinātar, iṉac cuṭṭillāp paṇpukoḷ peyarkkoṭi, vaḻakkāṟalla ceyyuḷāṟē (tol-kiḷavi- 18) eṉpataṉāl ivi muṭiyumāṟu aṟintu koḷka eṉa ittolkāppiya nūṟpāvukku uriyāciriyar kūṟiya ip poruḷic cuṭṭik kūṟiyuḷḷami iṅkuk karutiyuṇarat takuvatākum.
iṉi, iṉañcuṭṭi varum aṭimoḻi avviṭattiṟkup porutta muṭittāyiṉ iṉamallataṉiyum cuṭṭutal uṇṭu eṉpataṉi,
	401.	aṭimoḻi yiṉamal latuntaru māṇṭuṟiṉ.
eṉavarum nūṟpāviṟ pavaṇanti muṉivar kuṟippiṭuvar.
(u-m ) cumantāṉ viḻuntāṉ, putup puṉal vantatu, iruḷ pularntatu eṉpaṉa, cumakkappaṭṭatu viḻuntatu, maḻi peytatu, cuṭar tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉa muṟiyē viḷakkiṉavāṟu kāṇka eṉpar mayilinātar. iṉi, `pāvañ ceytāṉ narakam pukum eṉṟa tuṇiyāṉē puṇṇiyañ ceytāṉ cuvarkkam pukum eṉa iṉattit tarutalē yaṉṟi, `avaṉ itu ceyyiṉ itu varum eṉṉum aṟivili eṉṉum iṉa mallataṉiyum tantatu. mēlic cēric cēval alittatu eṉṟa tuṇiyāṉē kīḻic cēric cēval alippuṇṭatu eṉa iṉattit tarutalēyaṉṟi ac cēvaluṭiyārkku veṟṟiyāyiṟṟu eṉa iccūttirattiṟkuc caṅkara namaccivāyar kūṟum maṟṟoru viḷakkamum iṅku niṉikkat takuvatākum.
		vitanta moḻiyiṉam vēṟuñ ceppum
eṉṟār parimāṇaṉār.
	19.	iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal.
iyaṟkip poruḷmēl col nikaḻumāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) (taṉṉiyalpil tiriyātu) iyalpāki vārā niṉṟa poruḷi (ataṉ iyalpu kūṟuṅkāl ākkamum kāraṇamuṅ koṭātu) ittaṉmittu eṉṟu colluka. eṉṟavāṟu.
iyalpāvatu poruṭkup piṉ tōṉṟātu uṭaṉi kaḻuntaṉmi.
(u-m) nilam valitu, nīr taṇṇitu, tī veytu, vaḷi uḷarum, uyir uṇarum eṉavarum.
iṟṟu eṉpatu viṉikkuṟippu vāypāṭāyiṉum uḷarum uṇarum eṉṉum teriniliviṉiyum `iṟṟu eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa varutaliṉ, iṟṟeṉak kiḷattalēyām.
`nilam valitu āyiṟṟu eṉa ākkam peṟṟu variṉ, at toṭariṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa nilam eṉpatu iyaṟkip poruḷāta laṉṟik kallum cuṇṇāmpum pōlvaṉa peytu kuṟṟuc ceyyappaṭṭa ceyaṟkip poruḷātal vēṇṭum. iṉi nīr nilamum cēṟṟu nilamum muṉ mitittuc ceṉṟu vaṉṉilam mitittāṉoruvaṉ, `nilam valitāyiṟṟu eṉṟavaḻi, `muṉ melitāyatu piṉ valitāy vēṟupaṭṭatu eṉa ākkam vēṟupāṭu kuṟittu niṟṟaliṉ, iyaṟkip poruḷ ākkamoṭu vantataṉṟām eṉa viḷakkaṅ kūṟuvar cēṉāvariyar. pavaṇanti muṉivar innūṟpāviṉi,
	403.	iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal.
eṉat tāṉeṭuttu moḻital eṉṉum uttiyāl naṉṉūlil eṭuttāṇṭuḷḷār.
	20.	ceyaṟkip poruḷi yākkamoṭu kūṟal.
ceyaṟkip poruḷmēṟ col nikaḻumāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ceyaṟkiyiṉākiya poruḷi (ttiripu kūṟuṅkāl) ākkaṅ koṭuttuc colluka. eṉṟavāṟu.
ceyaṟkip poruḷāvatu ētēṉum oru kāraṇattāl taṉ taṉmi tirinta poruḷ. ākkamoṭu kūṟal eṉpataṉāl tiripu kūṟutal peṟṟām. eṉṉi? ataṉ kaṇallatu vārāmiyiṉ. iyaṟkip poruḷ, ceyaṟkip poruḷ eṉpaṉa iru peyaroṭṭup paṇput toki.
	21.	ākkan tāṉē kāraṇa mutaṟṟē.
itu, mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi yuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) (aṅṅaṉam ceyaṟkip poruḷōṭu iyitturikkap paṭum) ākkac col kāraṇatti mutalākavuṭiyatākum. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) eruppeytu iḷaṅkaḷikaṭṭu nīrkāl yāttami yāṟ payir nallavāyiṉa; kaṭukkalanta kipiḻi yeṇṇey peṟṟamiyāṉ mayir nallavāyiṉa eṉavarum. iyaṟkip poruḷ ākkamum kāraṇamum peṟātu varutalum ceyaṟkip poruḷ avipeṟṟu varutalum ilakkaṇameṉavē, ivvāṟaṉṟi varuvaṉa marapu vaḻuveṉpatām.
	22.	ākkak kiḷavi kāraṇa miṉṟiyum
pōkkiṉ ṟeṉpa vaḻakki ṉuḷḷē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiyatu.
(i-ḷ) (kāraṇa mutaṟṟu eṉappaṭṭa) ākkac col vaḻakkiṉuḷ kāraṇamiṉṟi variṉum kuṟṟamiṉṟu eṉpar. eṉṟavāṟu. pōkku - kuṟṟam.
(u-m) payir nallavāyiṉa, mayir nallavāyiṉa eṉavarum. vaḻakkiṉuṭ kāraṇamiṉṟiyum varumeṉavē ceyyuḷuṭ kāraṇam peṟṟē varum eṉpatām.
`ceyyuḷuḷ eṉak kiḷantōtātavaḻi avviti ulaka vaḻakkiṉiyē nōkkumātaliṉ, iccūttirattil `vaḻakkiṉuḷ eṉa vitanturittal vēṇṭā. ammikutiyāṉ, ceyaṟkip poruḷ ākkamum kāraṇamum iṉṟi varutalum kāraṇam koṭuttu ākkamiṉṟi varutalum eṉa iraṇṭuṅ koḷḷappaṭum  eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
(u-m) piṅkūḻ nalla eṉa ākkamum kāraṇamu miṉṟiyum, `eruppeytu iḷaṅkaḷikaṭṭu nīrkāl yāttamiyāṟ piṅkūḻ nalla eṉa ākkamiṉṟiyum vantaṉa.
kiḷaviyākkattil 20 mutal 22 variyuḷḷa mūṉṟu cūttiraṅ kaḷiṉ poruḷiyum 22-ām cūttirattiṟku uriyāciriyar kūṟiya viḷakkattiṉiyum vakippaṭutturikkum muṟiyil amintatu,
	404.	kāraṇa mutalā ākkam peṟṟum
kāraṇa miṉṟi ākkam peṟṟum
ākka miṉṟik kāraṇa maṭuttum
irumiyu miṉṟiyum iyaluñ ceyumporuḷ.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
kāraṇac colli muṉ koṇṭu ākkac colloṭu varuvaṉavum, kāraṇac colliṉṟi ākkac colloṭu varuvaṉavum, ākkac colliṉṟik kāraṇac colloṭu varuvaṉavum, ākkamum kāraṇamumiṉṟi varuvaṉavum eṉṉum innāṉku tiṟattil naṭakkum ceyaṟkip poruḷkaḷ eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
ceyum poruḷ iyalum eṉa viṉimuṭipuceyka. ceyum poruḷ - ceyaṟkip poruḷ.
	23.	pāṉ mayakkuṟṟa aiyak kiḷavi
tāṉaṟi poruḷvayiṟ paṉmi kūṟal.
itu tiṇiyuṇarntu pāl aiyan tōṉṟiyavaḻi col nikaḻttuvamāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) (tiṇituṇintu) pāl tuṇiyātu niṉṟa aiyap poruḷit tāṉaṟinta avvat tiṇip paṉmiyāṟ kūṟuka. eṉṟavāṟu.
kiḷavi eṉṟatu, īṇṭup poruḷi.
aiyap poruḷāvatu ciṟappiyalpāl tōṉṟātu potu viyalpāl tōṉṟiya poruḷ.
(u-m) oruvaṉ kollō orutti kollō āṇṭut tōṉṟuvār eṉavum, oṉṟō palavō ceypukkaṉa eṉavum varum.
tiṇivayiṉ eṉṉātu `tāṉaṟi poruḷ vayiṉ eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉamiyāl `oruvaṉ kollō palar kollō kaṟaviyuytta kaḷvar eṉavum, `orutti kollō palar kollō ik kurukkatti nīḻal vaṇṭala yarntār eṉavum tiṇiyoṭu āṇmi peṇmi tuṇinta paṉmiyorumip pāliyamum koḷḷappaṭum.
orumiyāṟ kūṟiṉ vaḻuvātal karutip paṉmi kūṟal eṉa vaḻānili pōlak kūṟiṉārāyiṉum orumiyip paṉmi yāṟ kūṟutalum vaḻuvātalāl aiyapporuḷmēl col nikaḻttumāṟu kūṟiya mukattāṟ pāl vaḻuvamittatu ic cūttiram eṉpatu cēṉāvariyar karuttākum.
	24.	uruveṉa moḻiyiṉu maḵṟiṇip pirippiṉum
iruvīṟṟu murittē cuṭṭuṅ kāli.
tiṇiyiyattukkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇip pāliyattuk kaṇṇum col nikaḻumāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) (tiṉiyiyan tōṉṟiṉa vaḻi) vaṭivu eṉac collak karutiṉum orumiyum paṉmiyumākap pirikkappaṭum aḵṟiṇi yiyaṟ peyarākiya potuc coṟkaṇṇum ivviru kūṟṟiṉum karutiyuṇaruṅkāl aiyattip pulappaṭuttum potuvākiya taṉmi urittu. eṉṟavāṟu.
uruvu - vaṭivu. uruviṉum eṉṉātu uruveṉa moḻiyiṉum eṉṟamiyāl apporuḷavākiya vaṭivu, piḻampu, piṇṭam eṉpaṉavum koḷka.
(u-m) kuṟṟi kollō makaṉ kollō tōṉṟukiṉṟa uruvu eṉavarum. itu tiṇi aiyam. oṉṟu kollō pala kollō ceypukka peṟṟam eṉavarum. iḵtu aḵṟiṇip pāliyam.
oruvaṉ kollō palar kollō eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattu uyartiṇip paṉmi yorumip pāliyattiṟku urupu mutalāyiṉa ēlāmiyum, tiṇiyiyattiṟkum ēṉip pāli yattiṟkum ēṟpuṭimiyum cuṭṭiyuṇarka eṉpatu pōtarac cuṭṭuṅkāli eṉṟār. eṉpar cēṉāvariyar. cuṭṭutal - karututal.
uruveṉpatu uṭaluyirk kūṭṭap potumi yākiya makka ḷeṉṉum potumikku ēlātu uṭaliyē uṇarttu talāṉum, peṟṟam eṉpatu iyaṟpeyarāyiṉum orukāṟ collutaṟkaṇ oru pāl mēl nillātu irupālmēl niṟṟalāṉum vaḻuvamitiyā yiṟṟu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
	25.	taṉmi cuṭṭalu muritteṉa moḻipa
aṉmik kiḷavi vēṟiṭat tāṉa.
iḵtu aiyuṟṟa poruḷit tuṇinta vaḻic col nikaḻumāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) aiyattiṟku vēṟāyt tuṇiporuḷiṭattu aṉmic col apporuḷiṉ uṇmit taṉmiyic cuṭṭi niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar. eṉṟavāṟu.
vēṟiṭattāṉa aṉmikkiḷavi taṉmi cuṭṭalum urittu eṉa moḻipa eṉa iyittup poruḷurikka. vēṟiṭam eṉṟatu, aiyattiṟku vēṟāyt tuṇintu taḻīik koṇṭa poruḷi. `kuṟṟiyōmakaṉō eṉa aiyan tōṉṟiya niliyil makaṉ eṉṟu tuṇinta vaḻi, kuṟṟiyallaṉ makaṉ eṉavum, kuṟṟiyeṉṟu tuṇinta vaḻi makaṉaṉṟu kuṟṟi eṉavum, oruvaṉō oruttiyō iṅkut tōṉṟā niṉṟār eṉa aiyan tōṉṟiya niliyil oruvaṉ eṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi orutti yallaṉ oruvaṉ eṉavum, oruttiyeṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi oruvaṉallaḷ orutti eṉavum, oṉṟō palavō ceypukka peṟṟam eṉa aiyan tōṉṟiya niliyil oṉṟeṉṟu tuṇinta vaḻi palavaṉṟu oṉṟu eṉavum, palaveṉṟu tuṇinta vaḻi, oṉṟalla pala eṉavum tuṇiyappaṭum poruḷiṉkaṇṇē allāta taṉmiyi vittuc colluvar.
`kuṟṟiyallaṉ makaṉ eṉpuḻik `kuṟṟiyiṉ allaṉ eṉṟu aintaṉurupu viritturippar iḷampūraṇar. aiyuṟṟa niliyil maṟukkappaṭu poruḷiṉ aṉmittaṉmiyiṉic cuṭṭutaṟkuriya aṉmik kiḷavi, ataṟku vēṟākiya tuṇiyap paṭu poruḷmēl niṉṟu ataṉ meyt taṉmiyiṉic cuṭṭi niṟṟalum uṇṭu eṉpār, aṉmik kiḷavi vēṟiṭattāṉa taṉmi cuṭṭalum urittu eṉṟār. ummi iṟantatu taḻīiya eccamātalāl kuṟṟiyaṉṟu makaṉ eṉa maṟukkappaṭu poruḷākiya taṉṉiṭattu aṉmit taṉmiyic cuṭṭi niṟṟalum koḷka. eṉṉi? aṉmik kiḷavi vēṟiṭattāṉa taṉmi cuṭṭalum urittu eṉavē aṉmik kiḷavi taṉṉiṭattāṉa aṉmit taṉmiyic cuṭṭalum urittu eṉpatum koḷḷap paṭumātaliṉ eṉka.
iṉi `vēṟiṭam eṉpataṉit tuṇiyap paṭum poruṭku vēṟākiya maṟukkap paṭum poruḷ eṉak koṇṭu,
makaṉeṉṟu tuṇintavaḻik kuṟṟiyaṉṟu makaṉ eṉavum kuṟṟiyeṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi makaṉallaṉ kuṟṟi eṉavum, āṇ makaṉeṉṟu tuṇintavaḻip peṇṭāṭṭi yallaḷ āṇmakaṉ eṉavum, peṇṭāṭṭi yeṉṟa tuṇintavaḻi āṇmakaṉallaṉ peṇṭāṭṭi eṉavum, oṉṟeṉṟu tuṇintavaḻip palavalla oṉṟeṉavum, palaveṉṟu tuṇintavaḻi oṉṟaṉṟu pala eṉavum maṟukkappaṭum poruḷmēl aṉmik kiḷavi aṉmit taṉmiyic cuṭṭi niṉṟatu eṉa utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvar cēṉā variyar. maṟukkappaṭum poruḷiṉ allāta taṉmiyi yuṭiyatu tuṇiyappaṭum poruḷātalāl ataṉ meyt taṉmiyic cuṭṭum niliyil ataṉ kaṇṇē aṉmik kaḷaviyi vitturittalum poruntum eṉpatām.
tiṇi pālkaḷil aiyantōṉṟiya vaḻiyum tuṇinta vaḻiyum col nikaḻumāṟu uṇarttum immūṉṟu cūttirap poruḷiyum tokuttuk kūṟum niliyil amintatu,
	375.	aiyan tiṇipāl avvap potuviṉum
meyteri poruṇmēl aṉmiyum viḷampupa.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
tiṇimēl aiyantōṉṟiyavaḻi avvirutiṇikkum potuvākiya collāṉum, pālmēl aiyantōṉṟiyavaḻi avvap pālkaḷukkup potuvākiya collāṉum, iṉṉa teṉṟu tuṇinta poruḷmēl allāta taṉmiyi vittum colluvar āciriyar eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
(u-m) kuṟṟiyō makaṉō eṉat tiṇiyiyan tōṉṟiya vaḻik `kuṟṟiyō makaṉō aṅṅaṉan tōṉṟā niṉṟatu - tōṉṟā niṉṟāṉ eṉac ciṟappuc collāṟ kūṟiṉ tiṇi vaḻuvum viṉāvaḻuvumām. aviyoḻittuk `kuṟṟiyō makaṉō aṅṅaṉam tōṉṟā niṉṟavuruvu eṉap potuc collāṟ kūṟuka. aḵṟiṇiyākiya kuṟṟikkum uyartiṇiyākiya makaṉukkum uruvu potuppaṭa niṉṟamiyiṉ potuc collāy vaḻānili yāyiṟṟu. āṇ makaṉō peṇ makaḷō eṉa uyartiṇip pāliyan tōṉṟiyavaḻi `āṇ makaṉō peṇ makaḷō aṅṅaṉan tōṉṟā niṉṟāṉ - tōṉṟāniṉṟāḷ eṉac ciṟappuc collāṟ kūṟiṟ pāl vaḻuvum viṉāvaḻuvumām. avi yoḻittu `āṇmakaṉō peṇmakaḷō aṅṅaṉan tōṉṟā niṉṟār eṉap potuc collāṟ kūṟuka. āṇ peṇ iraṇṭallatu palar pāl eṉa vēṟoṉṟiṉmiyiṉ tōṉṟā niṉṟār eṉpatu avviru pāṟkum potuc collāy vaḻā niliyāyiṟṟu. oṉṟō palavō eṉa aḵṟiṇip pāliyan tōṉṟiya vaḻi `oṉṟō palavō icceypukkatu - pukkaṉa, eṉac ciṟappuc collāṟ kūṟiṟ pālvaḻuvum viṉāvaḻuvumām. aviyoḻittu `oṉṟō palavō ic ceypukka peṟṟam eṉap potuc collāṟ kūṟuka.
meyteri poruḷāvatu aiyattiṉīṅki ituveṉat tuṇinta poruḷākum. kuṟṟiyō makaṉō eṉa aiyantōṉṟiya niliyil meyterinta poruḷ kuṟṟiyeṉiṉ `makaṉaṉṟu eṉavum, makaṉeṉiṟ `kuṟṟiyallaṉ eṉavum, kūṟuka. āṇmakaṉō peṇmakaḷō eṉa aiyuṟṟa niliyil tuṇintaporuḷ āṇ makaṉ eṉiṟ `peṇmakaḷallaṉ eṉavum, peṇmakaḷeṉiṉ `āṇ makaṉallaḷ eṉavum kūṟuka. mey teriyāp poruṇmēl allāta taṉmiyiṉi vittu `makaṉallaṉ kuṟṟi eṉiṉ kuṟṟiyeṉṉum payaṉilikku eḻuvāytantu `makaṉallaṉ avvuruk kuṟṟi eṉak kūṟal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam kūṟavē coṟpalkutal eṉṉum viṭi vaḻuvām. avvaḻu vārātu `meyteri poruṇmēl aṉmiyum viḷampupa eṉṟār. eṉa viḷakkuvar caṅkara namaccivāyar, `vēṟiṭattāṉa eṉavarum tolkāppiyat toṭarkku `aiyattiṟku vēṟāyttuṇi poruḷiṭattu eṉa iḷampūraṇar kūṟiya uriyiṉi `mey teri poruḷmēl eṉap pavaṇanti muṉivar avvāṟē ēṟṟup pōṟṟiyuḷḷami iṅku niṉikkattakuvatākum.
	24.	aṭiciṉi mutaleṉa muṟimūṉṟu mayaṅkāmi
naṭipeṟ ṟiyalum vaṇṇac ciṉiccol.
ituvum oru collutal vaṉmi yuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) aṭi, ciṉi, mutal eṉappaṭṭa mūṉṟum muṟi mayaṅkāmal vaḻakkip porunti naṭappaṉa vaṇṇac ciṉic col. eṉṟavāṟu.
aṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷatu kuṇam. ciṉi eṉpatu uṟuppu. mutal eṉpatu avvuṟuppiṉiyuṭi yaḻutaṟporuḷ. vaṇṇac ciṉic col - vaṇṇac colloṭu toṭarnta ciṉic colliyuṭiya mutaṟcol. vaṭivu mutalākiya piṟa paṇpumuḷavēṉum vaṇṇap paṇpiṉatu vaḻakkup payiṟci nōkki vaṇṇac ciṉic col eṉṟār.
(u-m) peruntalic cāttaṉ, ceṅkālnāri eṉavarum. talipperuñ cāttaṉ: kālcennāri eṉa muṟi mayaṅkivariṉ marapu vaḻuvām. naṭi - vaḻakku. vaḻakkiṉuḷ mayaṅkātu varumeṉavē ceyyuḷuḷ mayaṅki varavumpeṟum eṉpatām.
aṭi ciṉi mutal, eṉa varūum mūṉṟum eṉṉātu, muṟi eṉṟataṉāṉ irukuṇam aṭukki mutaloṭu varutalum, irukuṇam aṭukkic ciṉiyoṭu varutalum koḷka.
		`mutaloṭu kuṇamiraṇ ṭaṭukkutal vaḻakkiyal
ciṉiyo ṭaṭukkal ceyyu ḷāṟē.
eṉpatu puṟaccūttiram.
(u-m) iḷam peruṅkūttaṉ eṉavarum vaḻakkiṉuḷ,
ciṟupintūvic ceṅkāṟpēṭi eṉavarum ceyyuṭ kaṇ. `iḷamperuṅ kūttaṉ eṉṟak kāl iḷami perumiyi nōkki niṉṟataṉṟu, kūttaṉ eṉṉum peyariyē nōkki niṉṟa teṉavuṇarka. eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. iccūttirap poruḷiyum ivvuri viḷakkattiyum aṭiyoṟṟiyamintatu,
	402.	aṭiciṉi mutalmuṟi yaṭitalum, īraṭi
mutalō ṭātalum vaḻakkiyal; īraṭi
ciṉiyoṭu ceṟitalum mayaṅkaluñ ceyyuṭkē.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
oṉṟic colluṅkāl, aṭi, ciṉi, mutal eṉak kiṭanta muṟiyē collutalum, iraṇṭu aṭiyi mutaluṭaṉē cērttuc collutalum vaḻakkiṉuḷ iyalpām; iraṇṭu aṭiyic ciṉiyoṭu cērttuc collutalum, vēṇṭiya vāṟu mayaṅkac collutalum ceyyuṭkaṇ iyalpām eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
(u-m) peruntalic cāttaṉ, ceṅkāl, nāri, neṭṭilit teṅku eṉavum, iḷam peru vaḻuti, ciṟukaruṅ kākki eṉavum vaḻakkiṉuḷ vantavāṟu. ciṟu pintūviyiṟ ceyiraṟac ceyta , karu neṭuṅkaṇ taruṅkāma nōyē eṉavum, perun tōṭ ciṟu maruṅkuṟ pēramarkkaṭ pēti eṉavum ceyyuḷuḷ mayaṅki vantaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ aṭiciṉi mutal muṟiyaṭitaloṉṟum vaḻuvaṟka eṉak kāttalum ēṉiya mūṉṟum avvāṟu nillāmiyiṉ vaḻuvamitiyum ākum.
	27.	oruvarik kūṟum paṉmik kiḷaviyum
oṉṟaṉik kūṟum paṉmik kiḷaviyum
vaḻakki ṉākiya vuyar coṟ kiḷavi
ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṟ collā ṟalla.
itu, vaḻīi yamiyumāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) (oruvaṉ orutti eṉṉum) oruvarik kūṟum paṉmic collum (aḵṟiṇiyuḷ) oṉṟaṉi uyartiṇip paṉmiyākac collum paṉmic collum vaḻakkiṉakattu uyarttuc collappaṭuñ collām. avi ilakkaṇa muṟiyāṟ collum neṟiyalla. eṉṟavāṟu.
uyarcol - uyarkkuñ col; viṉittoki. uyar coṟkiḷavi eṉpatu iru peyaroṭṭup paṇput toki. oruvaṉiyum avar vantār eṉpa. oruttiyiyum avar vantār eṉpa. oṉṟaṉiyum avar vantār eṉpa.
(u-m) yām vantēm, nīyir vantīr, avar vantār eṉavarum.
`ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṟ collāṟalla eṉṟataṉāṉ ilakkaṇa maṉmiyum, `vaḻakkiṉākiya uyar coṟ kiḷavi eṉṟataṉāṉ vaḻuvaṉmiyum kūṟiṉār. kūṟavē vaḻuvamiti yeṉṟavāṟām.
ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṟ collāṟalla eṉṟataṉāṉ ilakkaṇamiyum, `vaḻakkiṉākiya uyar coṟ kiḷavi eṉṟataṉāṉ vaḻuvaṉmiyum kūṟiṉār. kūṟavē vaḻuvamiti yeṉṟavāṟām.
ilakkaṇa maruṅkiṟ collāṟalla eṉṟa mikiyāl uyartiṇiyi aḵṟiṇi pōlac collutalum, aḵṟiṇiyi uyartiṇi pōlac collutalum uḷaveṉak koṇṭu,
(u-m) `eṉ pāvi vantatu, pōyiṟṟu eṉa oruttiyiyum, `eṉ aṉṉi vantāḷ, pōyiṉāḷ eṉa ōr āviṉiyum kātaṉ mikutiyāṉ ivvāṟu kūṟutaluṅ koḷka. eṉavum, iṉi `vaḻakkiṉākiya eṉṟataṉāṉ, kaṉṉiyeyil, kaṉṉiñāḻal eṉpaṉa koḷka eṉavum utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭi viḷakkuvar iḷampūraṇar.
ivvāṟu kātal uyarvu mutaliyavaṟṟāl pālum tiṇiyum mayaṅki vaḻaṅkutali,
	378.	uvappiṉum uyarviṉuñ ciṟappiṉuñ ceṟaliṉum
iḻippiṉum pāltiṇi yiḻukkiṉum iyalpē.
eṉavarum cūttirattāṟ kuṟittār naṉṉūlār.
uvappu, uyarvu, ciṟappu, ceṟal, iḻivu eṉṉum ivvin tiṭattum pālum tiṇiyum mayaṅki varutalum iyalpām eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
(u-m) taṉ putalvaṉi `eṉ ammi vantāḷ eṉpatu uvappiṉ āṇpāl peṇpālāyiṟṟu. oruvaṉi avar vantār eṉpatu uyarviṉ orumippāl palarpālāyiṟṟu. `tāyākit taliyaḷikkum taṇṭuṟiyūra eṉpatu ciṟappiṉ āṇpāl peṇpālāyiṟṟu. `eṉittuṇiyarāyiṉum eṉṉān tiṉit tuṇiyun, tērāṉ piṟaṉil pukal eṉpatu ceṟaliṟ palarpāl orumip pālāyiṟṟu. peṇvaḻic celvāṉi nōkki `ivaṉ peṇ eṉpatu iḻiviṉ āṇpāl peṇ pālāyiṟṟu. ōr āviṉi `eṉ ammi vantāḷ eṉpatu, uvappiṉ aḵṟiṇi uyartiṇiyāyiṟṟu. `centārp pacuṅ kiḷiyār eṉpatu uyarviṉāṉ aḵṟiṇi uyartiṇiyāyiṟṟu. `ēvavuñ ceykalāṉ tāṉtērāṉ avvuyir, pōōmaḷavu mōr nōy eṉpatu ceṟaliṉ uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyāyiṟṟu. `iravetir koḷḷā irukāṟ pacuvē, ariya viratam aṭaṅku talillār eṉpatu iḻippiṉ uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyāyiṟṟu.
	28.	celaviṉum varaviṉun taraviṉuṅ koṭiyiṉum
nilipeṟat tōṉṟum annāṟ collum
taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkki yeṉṉum
ammū viṭattum uriya veṉpa.
iḵtu, iṭa vaḻukkāttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) cellutal, varutal, tarutal, koṭuttal ākiya nāṉku toḻiṟkaṇṇum nilipeṟap pulappaṭāniṉṟa annāṉku collum, taṉmi muṉṉili paṭarkki eṉṉum ammūviṭat tiṟkum uriyavāy varum eṉṟavāṟu. ivviṉic coṟkaḷ nāṉkum vikutiyāṉaṉṟi mutaṉiliyāy niṉṟa niliyilēyē iṭaṅkuṟittu niṟṟaluṭimiyāl ammūviṭattum uriya eṉṟār āciriyar.
avi mūviṭattiṟkum uriyavāmāṟu piṉvarum iraṇṭu nūṟpākkaḷil viritturikkappeṟum.
	29.	avaṟṟuḷ
tarucol varucol āyiru kiḷaviyum
taṉmi muṉṉili āyī riṭatta.
itu, tarucol varucol eṉpaṉa iṭamuṇarttumāṟu uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) tarucollum varucollumākiya iraṇṭum taṉmi muṉṉiliyākiya iraṇṭiṭattiṟkum uriya eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē ivi paṭarkkikkaṇ nilipeṟat tōṉṟā eṉpatām.
(u-m) eṉakkut tantāṉ, niṉakkut tantāṉ eṉavum, eṉṉuḻi vantāṉ, niṉṉuḻi vantāṉ eṉavum ivi īriṭattum vantaṉa.
tarappaṭum poruḷi yēṟpāṉ tāṉum muṉṉiṉṟāṉum ākalāṉum,  varavutoḻil taṉkaṇṇum muṉṉiṉṟāṉ kaṇṇuñ ceṉṟu muṭiyatalāṉum, īṟṟaṉaṉṟi mutaṉiliyāl iv virucollum taṉmi muṉṉilik kuriyavāyiṉa. nilipeṟat tōṉṟum annāṟ collum, taṉmi, muṉṉili paṭarkki yeṉṉum, ammūviṭattum uriya; eṉa mūṉṟiṭattiṟkum variviṉṟi ām eṉavum koḷḷavittamiyāṉ, peruviṟala mararkku veṉṟi tanta eṉavum, tūṇṭil vēṭṭuvaṉ vāṅka vārātu eṉavum mayaṅki varuvaṉavum amikkappaṭum eṉpar cēṉāvariyar.
	30.	ēṉi yiraṇṭum ēṉi yiṭattē
iḵtu ēṉiyiru coṟkaḷum iṭamuṇarttumāṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cellutalum koṭuttalum ākiya coṟkaḷ paṭarkkiyiṭattiṟku uriyaṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) avaṉkaṭ ceṉṟāṉ; avaṟkuk koṭuttāṉ eṉavarum.
celavu toḻil paṭarkkiyāṉkaṭ ceṉṟuṟutalāṉum, koṭip poruḷ ēṟpāṉ paṭarkkiyāṉākalāṉum īṟṟāṉṉaṟi ivviru collum mutaṉiliyāl paṭarkkiyiṭat tiṟkuriya vāyiṉa.
immūṉṟu cūttirap poruḷiyum tokutturikkum muṟiyil amintatu,
	380.	taral varal koṭi celal cārum paṭarkki
eḻuvā yiraṇṭum eñciya vēṟkum.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
`taral, varal, koṭi, celal eṉṉum innāṉku collum paṭarkkikku ām. ivaṟṟuḷ muṟkūṟappaṭṭa taral varal eṉṉum iraṇṭum eñci niṉṟa taṉmikkum muṉṉilikkum ām eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
	31.	yāteva ṉeṉṉu māyiru kiḷaviyum
aṟiyāp poruḷvayiṟ ceṟiyat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, aṟiyāp poruḷmēṟ colnikaḻttum marapu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) yātu evaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭu collum (teḷiya) aṟiyappaṭāta poruḷiṭattu viṉavum viṉāvāy yāppuṟat tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) iccoṟkup poruḷ yātu; iccoṟkup poruḷ evaṉ eṉavarum.
evvakiyālum aṟiyappaṭāta poruḷpaṟṟi viṉāt tōṉṟātātaliṉ, iṅku aṟiyāp poruḷ eṉṟatu, potu vakiyāl aṟiyappaṭṭuc ciṟappuvakiyāl teḷiya aṟiyap paṭāta poruḷi.
yāvaṉ, yāvaḷ, yāvar, yā, yāvi, yāṇṭu, yāṅku eṉpaṉa tiṇiyum pālum iṭamum mutalākiya ciṟappuvaki yālum ciṟitaṟiyappaṭṭa poruḷaṉa vātaliṉ, avaṟṟik kūṟātu, vaḻakkiṉuḷ potuvakiyāl aṟiyāp poruḷvayiṉ viṉāvāy varum yātu evaṉ eṉṉum ivviraṇṭiṉiyē iṅku eṭuttōtiṉār āciriyar.
	32.	avaṟṟuḷ
yāteṉa varūum viṉāviṉkiḷavi
aṟinta poruḷvayiṉ aiyantīrtaṟkut
terinta kiḷavi yākalu murittē.
iḵtu iṟantatu kāttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟkuṟitta avviru coṟkaḷuḷ yātu eṉpatu aṟiyapṭṭa poruṭkaṇ tōṉṟiya aiyattiṉi nīkkutaṟku ārāynta collātalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu. aṟinta poruḷ - potuvakiyāṉaṉṟic ciṟappu vakiyālum aṟiyappaṭṭa poruḷ.
(u-m) nam erutintaṉuḷ yātu keṭṭatu? eṉavarum. ummi - etirmaṟi; aṟiyāp poruḷvayiṉ ceṟiyat tōṉṟu talē peruvaḻakku eṉpatām.
yātu evaṉ eṉṉum viṉāc coṟkaḷi āḷutal paṟṟiya ivviti, curukka nūlātalpaṟṟi naṉṉūlil iṭampeṟṟilatu.
	33.	iṉitteṉa vaṟinta ciṉimutaṟ kiḷavikku
viṉippaṭu tokutiyiṉ ummi vēṇṭum.
itu, variyaṟiyuṭiyatu toki peṟuṅkāl varuvatōr marapu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) (kēṭpōrāl) ittuṇiyeṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭa ciṉik kiḷavikkum mutaṟ kiḷavikkum viṉippaṭu tokutikkaṇ ummi koṭuttuc colluka. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) nampi kaṇṇiraṇṭum civantaṉa; tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntarum vantār eṉavarum. viṉiyō aṉṟip peyarō muṭikkuñ colloṭu kūṭṭi muṭikkappaṭutali īṇṭu viṉippaṭu tokuti yeṉṟātātaliṉ, kaṇṇiraṇṭum kuruṭu, erutiraṇṭum mūri eṉa ummipeṟṟup peyar koṇṭu muṭivaṉavum iccūttiravitiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
ciṟappuṭip poruḷit tāṉiṉitu kiḷattal eṉpataṉāṟ ciṉimutaṟ kiḷavi yeṉṟārāyiṉum, ciṟavāta paṇpu mutalā yiṉavum iṉitteṉa vaṟintavaḻi ummipeṟutal koḷḷappaṭum.
(u-m) 
		`cuviyāṟum uṭittiv vaṭicil
`katiyintu muṭittik kutiri
eṉavarum.
	34.	maṉṉāp poruḷum aṉṉa viyaṟṟē.
ituvum oṉṟaṟku varuvatōr marapu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) illāp poruḷum (iṭamuṅkālamum poruḷu mutalāyiṉavaṟṟuṭaṉ cērttu iṉmi kūṟutaṟkaṇ) ummi koṭuttuc collap peṟutalākiya avviyalpiṉi yuṭiyatākum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) `maṉṉāp poruḷ eṉpataṟku `illāp poruḷ eṉap poruḷ koṇṭu, pavaḷak kōṭṭu nīlayāṉi cātavākaṉaṉ kōyiluḷḷumilli eṉa utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvar iḷampūraṇarum cēṉāvariyarum.
maṉṉāmi niliyāmiyi uṇarttutalaṉṟi illā miyi yāṇṭum uṇarttāmi kaṇṭa nācciṉārkkiṉiyar maṉṉāp poruḷ - ulakattu niliyillāta poruḷ eṉap poruḷ koṇṭu `yākkiyum niliyātu eṉa utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭi, yākkiyum niliyātu eṉavē iḷamiyum niliyātu celvamum niliyātu eṉṉum poruḷ uṇartti eccavummi yāy niṟkum eṉavum, ummipeṟuta lop pumiyāṉ muṟṟummiyōṭu eccavummiyi māṭṭeṟintār eṉavum iccūttirap poruḷi viḷakkuvar.
maṉṉutal poruttamum maṉṉāmi poruntāmiyum eṉap poruḷ koṇṭa teyvac ciliyār, `maṉṉāp poruḷum aṉṉaviyaṟṟē eṉavarum innūṟpāvukku poruntum poruḷum poruntāp poruḷum uṟaḻa vēṇṭumiṭattum, poruntum poruḷ kūṟumiṭattum ummi koṭuttuc colla vēṇṭum eṉap putiyatōr poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
vētākamat tuṇivu oruvarkku uṇarttumiṭattu, ulakum, uyirum, paramum aṉāti, patiyum, pacuvum, pācamum aṉāti eṉavarum. ulakum uyirum, paramum pacuvum poruntum poruḷ āṉavāṟum; pācamum patiyum ivaṟṟoṭu poruntāta poruḷ āṉavāṟum kāṇka. `uṭampum uyirum vāṭiyak kaṇṇum eṉavum, `nōyum iṉpamum eṉavuṅ kūṟiyavāṟum kāṇka eṉpatu innūṟpāvukkut teyvac ciliyār kāṭṭiya eṭuttuk kāṭṭum viḷakkamum ākum.
33, 34 ākiya ivviru cūttirapporuḷiyum ivaṟṟukku aminta iḷampūraṇar uriyiyum tokutturikkum muṟiyil amintatu,
	398.	iṉitteṉ ṟaṟiporuḷ ulaki ṉilāpporuḷ
viṉippaṭut turippiṉ ummi vēṇṭum.
eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
ittuṇit teṉṟu variyaṟuttu uṇarttappaṭum poruḷāti epporuḷkaḷum ulakiṉkaṇ illāta poruḷkaḷum viṉiyoṭu kūṭṭic colluṅkāli ummi koṭuttuc colluka eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
iccūttirattil varum `ulakiṉilāp poruḷ eṉṉun toṭar iḷampūraṇar urikkup porunta `ulakiṉ illāp poruḷ eṉavum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urikkup porunta `ulakiṉ nillāp poruḷ eṉavum pirittaṟkēṟṟa niliyil amintiruttal aṟintu makiḻat takuvatākum.
	35.	epporu ḷāyiṉum alla tilleṉiṉ
apporu ḷallāp piṟituporuḷ kūṟal.
itu, maṟittuc coṟpalkāmit tokuttiṟukkum ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) evvakippaṭṭa poruḷāyiṉum taṉṉuḻiyuḷḷa tallaṉi illiyeṉal uṟumēyaṉiṉ avaṉ kūṟiya poruḷallāta (ataṟkiṉamāyuḷḷa) piṟituporuḷ kūṟi illi eṉka. eṉṟavāṟu.
taṉṉuḻi uḷḷataṉ uṇmi kūṟi, avaṉ viṉaviya poruḷiṉ illāmiyi yuṇarttuka, eṉpatām.
(u-m) `payaṟuḷavō vaṇikīr eṉa viṉaviṉāl, avaṉ viṉaviya payaṟu taṉpāl illiyāyiṉ, ataṟkiṉamāyt taṉ pāluḷḷa piṟitākiya uḻuntiṉuṇmiyip pulappaṭuttum muṟiyil `uḻuntallatilli eṉa maṟumoḻi kūṟuka eṉpatām.
`allatilleṉiṉ eṉap poruḷ paṟṟi yōtiṉā rākaliṉ allatilleṉum vāypāṭēyaṉṟi, uḻuntaṉṟiyilli. uḻuntē yuḷḷatu eṉa apporuḷ paṭuvaṉa ellāṅ koḷka `piṟitu poruḷ eṉpataṟku `yātāṉum oru poruḷ  eṉṟār cēṉāvariyar.
`payaṟuḷavō vaṇikīr eṉa viṉaviṉārkku aḵtilli yeṉpāṉ, ap payaṟṟōṭotta iṉap poruḷākiya uḻuntu mutaliya tāṉiyaṅkaḷic cuṭṭi uḻuntallatilli, koḷḷalla tilli eṉa ivvāṟu kūṟutalē muṟi. ivvāṟaṉṟip `payaṟuḷavō vaṇikīr eṉa viṉaviṉārkku ataṉoṭu toṭarpillāta vakiyil `pāmpuṇik karuṅkallallatilli eṉak kūṟiṉ payaṟṟāl muṭikkuṅkuṟi ap pāmpuṇik karuṅkallāl muṭittalākāmiyiṉ poruntātu eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
	36.	apporuḷ kūṟiṟ cuṭṭik kūṟal.
ituvum ōr iṟuttal vakimi uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) allatil leṉpāṉ oruvar viṉāyiṉ ap poruḷiyē collaluṟumē yeṉiṉ (iṉṉa teṉac) cuṭṭik kūṟuka. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) iviyallatu payaṟilli; ippayaṟallatilli, eṉavarum. viṉāyiṉāṉ payaṟṟiṉ naṉmiyum tīmiyum uṇarntu ataṉik kōṭaṟkum tavirtaṟkum vēṇṭi, avaṉ viṉāya poruḷic cuṭṭik kūṟuka eṉṟār āciriyar. paṇṭamāṟṟiṉ kaṇ coṟpalkāmip poruṭṭuc curuṅkak kūṟum muṟiyiṉi aṟivuṟuttum ivviru cūttirap poruḷiyum taḻuvi amintatu,
	405.	tampā lilla tilleṉiṉ iṉaṉā
uḷḷatu kūṟi māṟṟiyum, uḷḷatu
cuṭṭiyum urippar coṟ curuṅkutaṟkē.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
oruvaṉ oṉṟi viṉāviṉāl atu tampakkal illi yeṉṟu urikka vēṇṭiṉ, avviṉāyataṟku iṉamāyt taṉpakkal uḷḷataṉic colli viṉāyataṉi illiyeṉṟum, uḷatāyiṉ ataṉi ittuṇiyuṇṭeṉṟu cuṭṭiyum colluvar tollōr, uri palkāmikku eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
	36. 	poruḷoṭu puṇarāc cuṭṭuppeya rāyiṉum
poruḷ vēṟu paṭāa toṉṟā kummē.
ituvum oru collutal vakimi uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) poruḷoṭu puṇarātu niṉṟa cuṭṭup peyarāmē yeṉiṉum, cuṭṭuvatōr poruḷiṭattu vēṟupaṭa nillāmal apporuḷiyē uṇartti niṟkum. eṉṟavāṟu.
poruḷoṭu puṇarāc cuṭṭuppeyar eṉṟatu, poruḷ iṭam kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻil eṉpaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi varintuṇarttātu ellāp poruṇmēlum cellum taṉmiyil aminta itu ivi mutaliya cuṭṭup peyarkaḷi. `ippayaṟu `ikkutiri eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa poruḷoṭu puṇarnta cuṭṭup peyarkaḷām.
(u-m) `payaṟuḷavō vaṇikīr eṉa viṉāyiṉārkku, `iviyallatu illi eṉa oruvaṉ cuṭṭik kūṟiya vaḻi `ivi eṉṉum cuṭṭu, poruḷoṭu puṇarāc cuṭṭup peyarākum.
`ippayaṟallatu illi eṉap poruḷōṭu puṇarttic cuṭṭik kūṟavēṇṭiyavaṉ `iviyallatu illi eṉac cuṭṭiya vaḻi, muṉkiṭanta payaṟukāṭṭi, `ivi eṉṟāṉākaliṉ, ivi eṉṉum cuṭṭu `ippayaṟu eṉa apporuḷi cuṭṭi niṟṟal kāṇka.
yāṉi nūl vallāṉoruvaṉ kāṭṭuṭ pōntapoḻutu ōr yāṉiyiṉ aṭiccuvaṭṭiṉik kaṇṭu, `iḵtu paṭṭattu yāṉi yātaṟ kēṟṟa ilakkaṇamuṭittu eṉṟa niliyil `iḵtu eṉpatu iṉṉa poruḷeṉap poruḷoṭu puṇarāta cuṭṭāyp palavaṟṟukkum potuvāy niṟpiṉum, colluvāṉ kuṟippāl `ivvaṭic cuvaṭṭukkuriya yāṉi eṉṟa poruḷi yuṇartti niṟṟalāl, poruḷ vēṟākātu ivyāṉiyeṉac cuṭṭikkūṟiya poruḷāy amintu niṟṟal kāṇka.
	38.	iyaṟpeyark kiḷaviyuñ cuṭṭuppeyarkkiḷaviyum
viṉikkoruṅ kiyaluṅ kālan tōṉṟiṟ
cuṭṭuppeyark kiḷavi muṟpaṭak kiḷavār
iyaṟpeyar vaḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu oru poruṇmēl iru peyar varuṅkāl varuvatoru marapu vaḻānili yuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) iyaṟpeyaruñ cuṭṭuppeyarum oṉṟaṉi oṉṟu koḷḷātu iraṇṭum piṟitu viṉikōṭaṟku oruṅku nikaḻuṅ kālan tōṉṟumāyiṉ (ulakattār) cuṭṭup peyari muṟpaṭak kūṟār (cuṭṭup peyarākiya accoṟkaḷ) iyaṟpeyarkkup piṉṉākak kūṟaṟpālaṉa eṉṟu colluvar pulavar. eṉṟavāṟu.
iyaṟpeyarāvaṉa koṟṟaṉ yāṉi eṉṟāṅku iru tiṇik kaṇṇum avvap poruḷikkuṟittu iyalpāy vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷ. iyaṟpeyareṉavē irutiṇikkaṇṇum iyaṉṟu varum peyarellām aṭaṅkum. cuṭṭup peyarāvaṉa avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi, ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, varum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ. viṉiyeṉṟatu muṭikkuñcolli. viṉikku oruṅku iyalutalāvatu, iyaṟpeyarum cuṭṭup peyarum oru poruḷiyē kuṟitta niliyil taṉittaṉiyākavum cērntum urupēṟṟum viṉimuṭipiṉip peṟṟuvarutal. vaḻiya - piṉṉiṭattaṉa. vaḻi- piṉ. iyaṟpeyarum cuṭṭup peyarumākiya avviru peyarum oruṅku viṉimuṭipiṉip peṟṟuvaruṅkālan tōṉṟiṉ iyaṟpeyari muṟkūṟic cuṭṭuppeyari av viyaṟpeyariṉ piṉṉark kūṟuka eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉāl aṟivuṟuttappeṟum vitiyākum.
(u-m) cāttaṉ avaṉ vantāṉ; cāttaṉ vantāṉ avaṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, cāttivantāḷ avaṭkup pūk koṭukka eṉavum varum. ivvāṟaṉṟi avaṉ vantāṉ cāttaṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉac cuṭṭup peyari iyaṟpeyarkku muṟkūṟiṉ, avaṉ eṉṉuñ cuṭṭu cāttaṉic cuṭṭātu cāttaṉallāta maṟṟoruvaṉic cuṭṭiyatāy iyaṟpeyarum cuṭṭup peyarum oru poruḷik kuṟittaṉa allavāy vēṟupaṭum eṉka.
oru poruḷik kuṟitta iyaṟpeyarum cuṭṭup peyarum piṟitu viṉi kōṭaṟkaṇ iyaṟpeyar muṉṉāka, ataṉ piṉ cuṭṭuppeyar varutal muṟiyeṉavē, ivviru peyarum avaṉ cāttaṉ, cāttaṉ avaṉ eṉa oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu payaṉiliyātaṟ kaṇṇum, avaṉum cāttaṉum vantār; cāttaṉum avaṉum vantār eṉa vēṟu poruḷik kuṟittaṉavāy varutaṟ kaṇṇum ivaṟṟuḷ etaṉi mutaṟkaṇ kūṟiṉum amiyum eṉpatām.
	39.	muṟpaṭak kiḷattal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) iyaṟpeyarum cuṭṭuppeyarum viṉikku oruṅku iyalum vaḻic cuṭṭup peyari muṟpaṭak kūṟutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) avaṉaṇaṅku nōy ceytāṉ āyiḻāyvēlaṉ viṟaṉmikutārc cēntaṉpēr vāḻtti - mukaṉamarn taṉṉi yalarkaṭappan tāraṇiyi leṉṉikol piṉṉi yataṉ kaṇ viḷivu eṉavarum itaṉuḷ avaṉ eṉpatu iyaṟpeyar. iyaṟpeyarkkumuṉ cuṭṭuppeyar varutal vaḻuvāyiṉum ceyyuḷātaliṉ amittukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu.
	40.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya kāraṇak kiḷaviyuñ
cuṭṭuppeya riyaṟkiyiṟ ceṟiyat tōṉṟum.
itu vaḻakkiṉkaṇ marapuvaḻāniliyum ceyyuṭkaṇ vaḻuvamiti yum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) cuṭṭi mutalākavuṭiya kāraṇap poruṇmiyi yuṇarttuñ collum, cuṭṭup peyariyottut taṉṉāṟ cuṭṭappaṭum poruḷiyuṇarttuñ coṟkup piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) cāttaṉ kiyeḻutumāṟu vallaṉ; ataṉāṟ ṟantiyuvakkum; cātti cāntarikkumāṟu vallaḷ, ataṉāṟ koṇṭāṉuvakkum eṉavarum.
muṉ cuṭṭup peyar eṉṟatu, poruḷvaḻi vanta cuṭṭiṉi eṉavum, iṅkuc cuṭṭu mutalākiya kāraṇak kiḷaviyeṉṟatu poruḷatu kuṇattiṉ vaḻivanta cuṭṭiṉi eṉavum pakuttuṇart tuvar iḷampūraṇar. iṅkuc cuṭṭu mutalākiya kāraṇak kiḷavi eṉṟatu, cuṭṭiṉi mutalākak koṇṭu ētupporuḷil vanta `ataṉāṉ eṉṉum colliṉi. iccol ataṉi ataṟku eṉṟāṟpōṉṟu ataṉāṉ eṉa mūṉṟāmmurupu ēṟṟu niṉṟa cuṭṭuppeyarōṭoppatōr iṭic collākum. iḵtu iyaṟpeyar poruḷic cuṭṭi niṟkum cuṭṭup peyar pōlātu, toṭarmoḻip poruḷic cuṭṭi niṟṟaliṉ, cuṭṭup peyariṉ vēṟeṉavē koḷḷappaṭum eṉpār `cuṭṭup peyariyaṟkiyiṟ ceṟiyat tōṉṟum eṉṟār āciriyar. `ceyaṟku eṉṉum viṉiyeccam, (ceyal + ku = ceyaṟku eṉa) urupēṟṟu niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyarōṭu oppumi yuṭittāyiṉum, pakuppap piḷavu paṭṭiciyātu oṉṟu paṭṭicittalāṉ, ataṉiṉ vēṟāyiṉāṟ pōla, ituvum urupēṟṟa cuṭṭup peyarōṭu oppumiyuṭit tāyiṉum piḷavu paṭṭiciyātu oṉṟu paṭṭicittalāṉ vēṟākavē koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar cēṉāvariyar.
cuṭṭup peyariyaṟki eṉṟatu, vaḻakkiṉattuc cuṭṭappaṭum poruḷiyuṇarttuñ coṟkup piṉṉiṟṟalum ceyyuḷāyiṉ orōvaḻi muṉṉiṟṟalum ākiya avviyalpiṉi.
taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl cuṭṭu mutalākiya kāraṇak kiḷavi pōṉṟu toṭarmoḻip poruḷic cuṭṭivarum cuṭṭup peyarum vaḻakkiṉakattuc cuṭṭappaṭum poruḷi yuṇarttuñ coṟkup piṉṉiṟṟalum ceyyuḷāyiṉ orōvaḻi muṉṉiṟṟalum koḷḷappaṭum.

(u-m) cāttaṉ vantāṉ, aḵtu aracaṟkut tuppāyiṟṟu eṉa vaḻakkiṉkaṇ piṉṉarum,
		`ākkamuṅ kēṭum ataṉāl varutalāṟ
kāttōmpal colliṉkaṭ cōrvu.
(tirukkuṟaḷ - 642)
eṉac ceyyuṭkaṇ muṉṉarum vantami kāṇka.
mēṟkuṟitta mūṉṟu cūttirap poruḷiyum tokuttu vakipeṟa viḷakkum muṟiyil amintatu,
	393.	paṭarkki muppeyarō ṭaṇiyiṟ cuṭṭup
peyarpiṉ varum viṉiyeṉiṟ peyarkkeṅkum
maruvum vaḻakkiṭic ceyyuṭ kēṟpuḻi.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
paṭarkki yiṭattaṉavākiya uyartiṇip peyar, aḵṟiṇip peyar, viravuppeyar eṉṉum mūvakip peyarōṭu cuṭṭup peyar viṉikkaṇ oruṅku varumāyiṉ aḵtu avviyaṟ peyariṉ piṉṉē varum; peyark kaṇṇāyiṟ piṉṉum muṉṉum varum vaḻakkiṉuḷ. ceyyuṭ kaṇṇāyiṉ iṉṉa viṭattu eṉṉum niyatiyiṉṟi appeyarkaḷuṭaṉē piṉṉum muṉṉum vēṇṭiya vāṟu varappeṟum eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
(u-m) nampi vantāṉ, avaṟkuc cōṟu koṭukka; erutu vantatu, ataṟkup pul iṭuka; cāttaṉ vantāṉ, avaṟkuc cōṟu koṭukka; cāttaṉ vantatu, ataṟkup pul iṭuka eṉa viṉikkaṭ cuṭṭuppeyar piṉṉē vantatu. cāttaṉ avaṉ, avaṉ cāttaṉ eṉap peyarkkaṇ īriṭattum vantatu. ivi vaḻakkiyal. ceyyuḷāyiṉ `avaṉaṇaṅku nōy ceytāṉ .......... cēntaṉ eṉa iyaṟ peyarkku muṉ varutalum amiyum. ivvāṟaṉṟi nampiyic cuṭṭiya niliyil `avaṉ vantāṉ, nampikkuc cōṟukoṭukka eṉac cuṭṭup peyari vaḻakkiṉuḷ muṟkiḷattal vaḻu eṉpatām.
	41.	ciṟappi ṉākiya peyarnilik kiḷavikkum
iyaṟpeyark kiḷavi muṟpaṭak kiḷavār.
ituvum, oruporuḷmēl irupeyar varuṅkāl varuvatoru marapuvaḻānili yuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) viṉikku oruṅku iyalumvaḻic ciṟappiṉākiya peyarkkum iyaṟ peyari muṟpaṭak kūṟār piṟpaṭak kūṟuvar ulakattār. eṉṟavāṟu.
viṉikku oruṅku iyaluṅkāl eṉpatu atikārattāl varuvitturikkappaṭṭatu. īṇṭuc ciṟappiṉākiya peyar eṉṟatu, maṉṉar mutalāyiṉārāṟ peṟum variciyiṉik kuṟitta ēṉāti, kāviti mutaliya ciṟappup peyarkaḷi.
(u-m) ēṉāti cāttaṉ, kāviti kaṇṇanti eṉavarum.
`peyar nilik kiḷavikkum eṉṟa ummiyāl, tavam, kalvi, uṟuppu mutalāyiṉavaṟṟāṉākiya peyarum iyaṟ peyarkku muṉṉark kiḷattal koḷḷappaṭum.
(u-m) muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ; teyvap pulavaṉ tiruvaḷḷuvaṉ; cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi; kuruṭaṉ koṟṟaṉ eṉavarum. iccūttirap poruḷi aṭiyoṟṟi yamintatu,
	392.	tiṇi nilañ cāti kuṭiyē yuṭimi
kuṇan toḻil kalvi ciṟappām peyarō
ṭiyaṟpeya rēṟṟiṭiṟ piṉvaral ciṟappē.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
tiṇip peyar mutalāṉa peyarkaḷuṭaṉē iyaṟpeyari iyittuc colluṅkāl, avviyaṟ peyari muṟ kuṟitta peyarkaḷukkup piṉṉē collutal ciṟappuṭittu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
(u-m) kuṉṟavaṉ kuṟavaṉ, aruvāḷaṉ aḻakaṉ, pārppāṉ māṭalaṉ, kañcāṟūrkiḻāṉ kaṇṇaṉ, meyyaṉ veṇkāṭaṉ, nāṭaki nampi eṉa muṟiyē tiṇi, nilam, cāti, uṭimi, kuṇam, toḻil paṟṟiya peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉāka iyaṟpeyar vantami kāṇka. ēṉiyavaṟṟukku utāraṇam muṉṉark kāṭṭap peṟṟaṉa.
	42.	oru poruḷ kuṟitta vēṟupeyark kiḷavi
toḻil vēṟu kiḷappiṉ oṉṟiṭa ṉilavē.
iḵtu oruporuḷmēṟ palapeyar varuṅkāl marapu vaḻāmik kākkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oru poruḷik kuṟittuvanta palapeyarc coṟkaḷ, oru toḻilē muṭipākak kūṟātu, peyartōṟum vēṟuvēṟākiya toḻilkaḷik koṭuttu muṭippiṉ, oruporuḷavāyp poruntum iṭaṉuṭiya alla eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē pala peyar aṭukkivariṉ oruviṉiyāṉ muṭittal vēṇṭum eṉpatām.
toḻil vēṟukiḷattalāvatu, oru poruḷ mēl varum pala peyarkaḷukkum orutoḻilē muṭipākak kūṟātu vēṟu vēṟu muṭikkuñ collit tantu kūṟutal, `āciriyaṉ pērūrkiḻāṉ ceyiṟṟiyaṉ iḷaṅkaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉ vantāṉ eṉa ivvāṟu oru toḻilē muṭipākak kūṟātu, `āciriyaṉ vantāṉ, pērūrkiḻāṉ uṇṭāṉ ceyiṟṟiyaṉ ceṉṟāṉ eṉa vēṟu vēṟu toḻil kiḷantavaḻi, vantāṉum uṇṭāṉum ceṉṟāṉum oruvaṉākātu vēṟuvēṟāvar eṉap poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu vaḻuvām ātaliṉ avvāṟu vaḻuvāmik kāttatu ic cūttiram eṉavuṇarka.
`enti varuka, emperumāṉ varuka, mintaṉ varuka, maṇāḷaṉ varuka eṉpuḻi, oruporuḷ kuṟitta palapeyar tōṟum kātal mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi oru tōḻilē palakāl vanta tallatu vevvēṟu toḻil vārāmiyāṉ, attoṭar oru toḻil kiḷantateṉavē koḷḷappaṭum.
iccūttirapporuḷi aṭiyoṟṟi yamintatu,
	391.	oruporuṇ mēṟpala peyarvari ṉiṟuti
oruviṉi koṭuppa taṉiyu morōvaḻi.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
oru poruḷiṉmēṟ palapeyarkaḷ aṭukkivariṉ, avaṟṟiṟkellām īṟṟilē oru viṉiyik koṭuppar. ciṟupāṉmi peyar tōṟum oru viṉiyik koṭuttum vaḻaṅkuvar periyōr eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. eṉavē peyartōṟum vēṟuvēṟu viṉiyic koṭuttal marapaṉṟu eṉpatām. orōvaḻi - ciṟupāṉmi.
(u-m) āciriyaṉ pērūrkiḻāṉ ceyiṟṟiyaṉ iḷaṅ kaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉ vantāṉ eṉavum, `aiyaṉ aṇintāṉ ēṉi āḷum aṇṇal aṇintāṉ, ārūriṟ civaṉ aṇintāṉ, eṉ tuṇiyām talivaṉ aṇintāṉ eṉavum varum.
iṉi, `entivaruka, emperumāṉ pōka, mintaṉ niṟka, matali irukka eṉap peyartōṟum vēṟu vēṟu viṉi koṭuppiṉ, appeyarkaḷ yāvum oruporuḷavāyp poruntā eṉpatām.
	43.	taṉmic collē yaḵṟiṇik kiḷaviyeṉ
ṟeṇṇuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ viravutal variyār.
itu, tiṇivaḻuk kākkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) taṉmic collum aḵṟiṇic collum eṇṇutaṟkaṇ viravi varutali nīkkār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
taṉmiccol uyartiṇic col. taṉṉik kuṟittuc col nikaḻttum colvaṉmi uyartiṇi māntarkkē yuriyatātalpaṟṟi uyartiṇic collit taṉmic col eṉṟār. uyartiṇic collum aḵṟiṇic collum eṇṇutaṟkaṇ viravi vantu uyartiṇi muṭipu koḷḷiṉum aṉṟi aḵṟiṇi muṭipu koḷḷiṉum vaḻuvā mātaliṉ `mayaṅkal kūṭā tam marapiṉavē eṉṉum vitiyāl avvirutiṇic coṟkaḷum viraviyeṇṇap paṭāmiyum uḷappaṭutti muṭittal vaḻuvāmākalāl taṉmic collum aḵṟiṇic collum viravi vantu uyartiṇi muṭipu koḷḷiṉum amiyum eṉa iccūttirattāl āciriyar tiṇivaḻuk kāttavāṟu.
(u-m) yāṉum eṉa eḵkamuñ cāṟum eṉavarum.
iṅṅaṉam taṉmic collum aḵṟiṇic collum viravi yeṇṇutaṟkaṇ avaṟṟi muṭikkuñcollāy varuvatu taṉmip paṉmic col eṉpatu paṉmiyurikkun taṉmik kiḷavi, eṇṇiyaṉmaruṅkiṟ ṟiripavi yuḷavē (tol - viṉiyiyal - 12) eṉa mēlvarum cūttirattāl iṉitu pulaṉām.
taṉmiyākiya uyartiṇiyic cārtti eṇṇappaṭum aḵṟiṇic collum taṉmiyākiya uyartiṇi muṭipu peṟum eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟiya ivvaḻuvamiti, tammic col irutiṇip potuc collāy māṟiya tam kālattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyātataṉṟu eṉak karutiya pavaṇantiyār, uyar tiṇiyic cārntuvarum poruḷ iṭam kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻil paṟṟiya aḵṟiṇic coṟkaḷ uyartiṇiyoṭu cārtti yurikkuṅkāl uyartiṇi muṭipiṉavāy vaḻaṅkum piṟkālattu vaḻakkiṉi amittuk koḷḷum muṟiyil,
	376.	uyartiṇi toṭarnta poruṇ mutalāṟum
ataṉoṭu cārttiṉ attiṇi muṭipiṉa.
eṉac cūttirañ ceytār.
uyartiṇiyic cārntu varum poruḷātiyāṟum av vuyartiṇiyoṭu cārtti muṭippiṉ uyartiṇi muṭipiṉavām eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
(u-m) nampi poṉperiyaṉ, nāṭu periyaṉ, vāḻnāḷ periyaṉ, kaṇperiyaṉ, kuṭimi nallaṉ, celavu nallaṉ eṉa uyartiṇi cārnta poruḷātiyāṟum uyartiṇimuṭipu peṟṟaṉa. ivvāṟu uyartiṇiyic cārttātu taṉittuk kūṟum vaḻi, nampikkup poṉ peritu, nāṭu peritu, vāḻnāḷ peritu, kaṇ peritu, kuṭimi naṉṟu, celavu naṉṟu eṉat tamakkuriya aḵṟiṇi muṭipē peṟum eṉpatām.
	44.	orumi yeṇṇiṉ potuppiri pāṟcol
orumik kalla teṇṇumuṟi nillātu.
itu marapu vaḻuk kākkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) orumiyeṇṇiṉi yuṇarttum potumi yiṉiṉṟum pirinta pāṟ coṟkaḷākiya oruvaṉ orutti eṉṉum coṟkaḷ irumi mutalākiya eṇṇu muṟikkaṇ nillā. eṉṟavāṟu.
orumiyeṇṇiṉiyuṇarttum potu eṉṟatu, uyartiṇikkaṇ āṇorumiyiṉiyum poṇṇorumi yiṉiyum uṇarttuvatāy, avvirupāṟkum potuvāy niṉṟa oruvar eṉṉum colliṉ ar vikuti. potuppiri pāṟcol eṉṟatu oruvar eṉap palarpāṟkuriya ar īṟṟatāy niṉṟa appotumiyiṉiṉṟum pirintu oruvaṉ eṉa āṇpāliyum orutti eṉap peṇpāliyum cuṭṭi niṟkum aṉ vikutiyiṉiyum ikara vikutiyiṉiyum potuppiri pāṟcollākiya ivaṟṟiṉ pāluṇarttum īṟākiya ar vikuti iruvarmūvar eṉa eṇṇu muṟikkaṇ niṟkum eṉpatām.
makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṉun toṭakkattup peyarp potup piri pāṟcolliṉ nīkkutaṟku `orumiyeṇṇiṉ eṉṟum, oruvar oṉṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ nīkkutaṟkup `potuppiripāṟ col eṉṟum aṭipuṇarttōtiṉār. oruvaṉ, orutti eṉpaṉa oru var eṉṉum āṇmip peṇmip potuviṉiṉṟum pirintu niṉṟu āṇpāliyum peṇpāliyum varintuṇarttuvaṉa vātaliṉ avi potuppiripāṟ collāyiṉa. `potuppiripāṟ col eṉac col eṉṉum oṟṟumiyāl `nillātu eṉa orumiyāṟ kūṟiṉār.
oruvaṉ orutti eṉa orumikkaṇ niṟṟalum, iruvaṉ, mūvaṉ, irutti, muttiyeṉa eṇṇu muṟikkaṇ nillāmiyum kaṇṭu koḷka eṉṟār cēṉāvariyar.
iccūttirap poruḷit teḷiya viḷakkum muṟiyil amintatu,
	287.	oruva ṉoruttip peyarmē leṇṇila.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl oruvēṉ, oruvi eṉṉun taṉmi muṉṉiliyīṟum eṇṇumuṟi nillāmi koḷka.
	45.	viyaṅkō ḷeṇṇuppeyar tiṇiviravu variyār
itu tiṇivaḻu amikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) viyaṅkōḷoṭu toṭarum eṇṇuppeyar tiṇivirāy varutali nīkkār (āciriyar).
iṅku eṇṇup peyar eṉṟatu tokipeṟṟē varum eṇṇallāta ummiyeṇṇum eṉaveṉ eṇṇum eṉak koḷvar iḷampūraṇar.
(u-m) āvum āyaṉum celka eṉavarum. celka eṉṉum viyaṅkōḷ irutiṇiyiṉiyum muṭittaṟkuriya potu viṉiyāy vaḻuvaṟṟatāyiṉum āvākiya aḵṟiṇi celka eṉṉum ēvaṟṟoḻili muṟṟamuṭiyāmi karuti vaḻuvamittār eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ivviti naṉṉūliṟ kūṟappeṟṟilatu.
	46.	vēṟuviṉip potuccol oruviṉi kiḷavār.
itu marapu vaḻuk kākkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) vēṟupaṭṭa pala viṉiyiṉiyuṭiya pala poruṭkup potuvākiya peyari oru viṉiyāṟkūṟār. eṉavē avaṟṟiyuḷḷaṭakki niṟkum potuviṉiyāṟ kūṟuvar eṉṟavaṟām.
vēṟu viṉip potuc collāvaṉa vēṟuvēṟu viṉikaḷiyuṭiya pala poruṭkum potuvāyaminta aṭicil, aṇikalam, iyam, paṭi mutaliya potuc coṟkaḷ. aṭicil eṉpatu uṇpaṉa tiṉpaṉa parukuvaṉa nakkuvaṉa eṉṉum nālvaki viṉikkum, aṇiyeṉpatu kavippaṉa kaṭṭuvaṉa ceṟippaṉa pūṇpaṉa eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum, iyam eṉpatu koṭṭuvaṉa ūtuvaṉa eḻuppuvaṉa eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum, paṭi eṉpatu eyvaṉa eṟivaṉa veṭṭuvaṉa kuttuvaṉa veṉṉun toṭakkattaṉavaṟṟiṟkum potuvākaliṉ avvāṟē aṭicil ayiṉṟār, micintār eṉavum aṇi aṇintār, meyppaṭuttiṉār eṉavum, iyam iyampiṉār, paṭuttār eṉavum, paṭi vaḻaṅkiṉār, toṭṭār eṉavum potu viṉiyāṟ kūṟuka eṉpatām. ivvāṟaṉṟi aṭicil tiṉṟār, parukiṉār; aṇi kavittār, pūṇṭār eṉa oru cārkkuriya vēṟu viṉiyāṟ colliṉ marapu vaḻuvām ātaliṉ iccūttirattāl marapu vaḻāmik kāttavāṟu.
	47.	eṇṇuṅ kālu matuvataṉ marapē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ) vēṟuviṉip poruḷkaḷip potuccollāṟ kūṟātu pirittu eṇṇumiṭattum ataṉ ilakkaṇam vēṟu viṉiyāṟkiḷavātu potuviṉiyāṟ kiḷattalē yām. eṉṟavāṟu.
eṇṇuṅkālum ataṉ marapu atu eṉa iyiyum.
(u-m) cōṟum kaṟiyum uṇṭār; yāḻuṅ kuḻalum iyampiṉār eṉavarum.
ivviru cūttirap poruḷiyum taḻuviyamintatu,
	388.	vēṟuviṉip palporuḷ taḻuviya potuccolum
vēṟavaṟ ṟeṇṇumōr potuviṉi vēṇṭum.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum. vevvēṟu viṉikaṭkuriya pala poruḷkaḷiyum taḻuviniṟkum ōr potuc collum, avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappuc coṟkaḷāy eṇṇappaṭṭu niṟkum pala collum oṉṟaṟkuriya ciṟappuviṉi vēṇṭātu ellāvaṟ ṟiṟkum uriyatōr potuviṉi vēṇṭuvaṉavām eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
	48.	iraṭṭik kiḷavi yiraṭṭiṟ pirin ticiyā.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ) iraṭṭittu niṉṟu poruḷuṇarttuñ coṟkaḷ iraṭṭittu niṟṟaliṟ pirintu nillā. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) curu curuttatu; moṭu moṭuttatu eṉa ici paṟṟiyum, koṟu koṟuttār; moṟu moṟuttār eṉak kuṟippup paṟṟiyum, kuṟukuṟuttatu; kaṟukaṟuttatu eṉap paṇpu paṟṟiyum iraṭṭittu vantaṉa pirintu nillāmi kaṇṭukoḷka. kaṟuttatu kaṟuttatu; kuṟuttatu kuṟuttatu eṉṟāṅkuc col muḻutum vārāmiyiṉ ivi aṭukkut toṭaraṉmi yaṟika. īṇṭu iraṭṭikkiḷavi eṉṟatu, makkaḷiraṭṭi vilaṅki raṭṭi pōla vēṟṟumi yuṭiyaṉa vaṟṟiyaṉṟi iliyiraṭṭiyum pūviraṭṭiyum pōla oṟṟumiyum vēṟṟumiyu muṭiyaṉavaṟṟi eṉa viḷakkam taruvar cēṉāvariyar.
iraṭṭittu niṉṟu poruḷuṇarttuvaṉavaṟṟip pirittu vaḻaṅkal marapaṉmiyiṉ, marapu vaḻukkāttavāṟu. innūṟ pāvi,
	395.	iraṭṭik kiḷavi yiraṭṭiṟpirin ticiyā.
eṉa ivvāṟē eṭuttāḷuvar pavaṇanti muṉivar.
	49.	oru peyarp potucco luḷporu ḷoḻiyat
teripu vēṟukiḷattal talimiyum paṉmiyum
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉum aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ) uyartiṇik kaṇṇum aḵṟiṇik kaṇṇum oru peyarppaṭa niṉṟa potuc coṟkaḷic colluṅkāl, aṅkuḷḷa poruḷellām eṭuttuc collātu, talimiyum paṉmiyum paṟṟi (avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi)t terintu koṇṭu vēṟākac colluka. eṉṟavāṟu.
cēri eṉpatu pala kuṭumpattār cērntu vāḻum iṭam. āyiṉum aṅkup pārppaṉakkuṭikaḷ cila mutaṉmipeṟṟu viḷaṅkutal paṟṟi ataṉip pārppaṉac cēri eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. itu talimi paṟṟiya vaḻak keṉappaṭum. iṉi aṅkup perum pāṉmiyarāka vāḻvōr vaṭukarātal paṟṟi ataṉi vaṭukac cēri eṉa vaḻaṅkutal uṇṭu. itu paṉmi paṟṟiya vaḻakkeṉappaṭum.
palamaraṅkaḷ tokkatu tōṭṭam. ataṉkaṇ piṟa pullum maramum uḷavāyiṉum aṅkuk kamuku mutaṉmiyāka vaḷarn tuḷḷami paṟṟik kamukan tōṭṭam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. itu talimi paṟṟiya vaḻakkeṉappaṭum. māmaraṅkaḷ niṟintuḷḷa tōṭṭa māyiṉ māntōṭṭam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. itu paṉmi paṟṟiya vaḻakkeṉappaṭum.
iṉi, pārppār palarāka vāḻumiṭattip pārppaṉaccēri yeṉavum, kamukukaḷ palavāka vuḷḷa tōṭṭattik kamukan tōṭṭam eṉavum vaḻaṅkiṉ atuvum paṉmi paṟṟiya vaḻakkeṉappaṭum.
īṇṭup potuc col eṉṟatu palaporu ḷorucol laṉṟic cēri tōṭṭam eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu oru poruḷ kuṟitta peyarē eṉpār, oru peyarp potuc col eṉa aṭi koṭuttōtiṉār. oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl potuc col allātaṉavum terintu vēṟu kiḷattal koḷḷappaṭum.
ōriṭattu uḷḷa poru ḷellām eṭutturiyātu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi maṭṭum terinteṭutturittal ilakkaṇa maṉṟāyiṉum marapu paṟṟi amittuk koḷka eṉak kūṟutaliṉ iccūttiram marapuvaḻuk kāttalāyiṟṟu.
	50.	peyariṉun toḻiliṉum piripavi yellām
mayaṅkal kūṭā vaḻakku vaḻip paṭṭaṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ) uyartiṇik kaṇṇum aḵṟiṇik kaṇṇum peyariṉālum viṉiyiṉālum potumiyiṟ pirintu āṇmikkum peṇmikkum uriyavāy varuvaṉa vellām vaḻu vākā; vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭaṉa vākalāṉ. eṉṟavāṟu.
mēṟ cūttirattiluḷḷa `uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉum aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉum eṉpatu atikārattāl vantiyintatu. peyariṉālum toḻiliṉālum piripavi eṉappaṭṭavi, irutiṇiyilum āṇ peṇ irupālkaḷuḷ peṇṇoḻi mikucollum āṇoḻi mikucollum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. peruntēvi karuvuyirtta kaṭṭiṟkīḻ nālvar paṇimakkaḷ uḷar eṉpuḻi makkaḷ eṉpatu peyariṟ pirinta āṇoḻi mikucol. tirutarāṭṭiraṉ makkaḷ nūṟṟuvariyuṭiyaṉ eṉpuḻi makkaḷ eṉpatu peyariṟ pirinta peṇṇoḻi mikucol. patiṉmar ivvayaliṟ kaḷipaṟittār eṉpuḻik kaḷipaṟittār eṉpatu toḻiliṟ pirinta āṇoḻimikucol. patiṉmar ivvayalil uḻutār eṉpuḻi uḻutār eṉpatu toḻiliṟ pirinta peṇṇoḻi mikucol. ivi uyartiṇikkaṇ peyariṉum toḻiliṉum pirintaṉa. ivvēntaṉ āyiram yāṉiyuṭiyaṉ eṉpuḻi yāṉi eṉpatu peyariṟ pirinta peṇṇoḻi mikucol. kaṇṇaṉ nūṟu erumiyuṭiyaṉ eṉpuḻi erumi eṉpatu peyariṟ pirinta āṇoḻi mikucol. ippeṟṟam uḻutaṉa eṉpuḻi uḻutaṉa eṉpatu toḻiliṟ pirinta peṇṇoḻi mikucol. ippeṟṟam niṟiyak kaṟakkum eṉpuḻik kaṟakkum eṉpatu toḻiliṟ pirinta āṇoḻi mikucol. ivi aḵṟiṇikkaṇ peyariṉum toḻiliṉum pirintaṉa. iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa peyarum toḻilum āṇ peṇ irupāṟkum potuvāyiṉum ivi piṟacollāṉaṉṟit tāmē pirintu kuṟippiṉāṉ orupāṟkaṇ vaḻaṅkutal ulakavaḻakkiṟ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ `mayaṅkal kūṭā vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭaṉa eṉṟār āciriyar.
ittolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ poruḷi viḷakkum muṟiyil amintatu,
	351.	irutiṇi yāṇpeṇu ḷoṉṟaṉi yoḻikkum
peyarum viṉiyuṅ kuṟippi ṉāṉē.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉṉum irutiṇiyilum āṇ peṇ irupāṟkum potuvāṉa peyarccollum viṉic collum avvirupāluḷ orupālik kuṟippiṉāl oḻikkum eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
	51.	palavayi ṉāṉu meṇṇuttiṇi viravuppeyar
aḵṟiṇi muṭipiṉa ceyyu ḷuḷḷē.
itu tiṇi vaḻu amikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) tiṇi viravi eṇṇappaṭṭa peyar ceyyuḷa kattup perumpāṉmiyum aḵṟiṇiccoṟ koṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
eṇṇuttiṇi viravup peyar - uyartiṇiyum aḵṟiṇiyumāka viravi eṇṇap paṭṭa peyarkaḷ; ceyyuḷuḷ palavayiṉāṉum aḵṟiṇi muṭipiṉa eṉa iyiyum. pala vayiṉāṉum - perum pāṉmiyum.
(u-m)
		vaṭukar aruvāḷar vāṉkaru nāṭar
cuṭukāṭu pēy erumi eṉṟivi yāṟum
eṉavum,
		kaṭuñciṉatta kolkaḷiṟuṅ kataḻ pariya kalimāvum
neṭuṅ koṭiya nimir tērum neñcuṭiya pukal maṟavarum eṉa
nāṉkuṭaṉ māṇṭatāyiṉum
eṉavum tiṇiviravi aḵṟiṇi muṭipu peṟṟaṉa.
eṇṇut tiṇi viravup peyar perum pāṉmiyum aḵṟiṇic coṟ koṇṭu muṭiyumeṉavē, ciṟupāṉmi uyartiṇic coṟ koṇṭu muṭiyavum peṟum eṉpatām.
(u-m)
		pārppār aṟavōr pacuppattiṉip peṇṭir
mūttōrkuḻavi eṉumivarik kiviṭṭu
eṉavum
		pārppār tavarē cumantār piṇippaṭṭār
mūttār iḷiyār pacup peṇṭir eṉṟivarkaṭku
eṉavum tiṇi viravic ciṟupāṉmi uyartiṇic coṟ koṇṭu muṭintaṉa. tiṇi virā yeṇṇi aḵṟiṇiyāṉum uyartiṇi yāṉum muṭintatu, talimi paṟṟiyum, paṉmi paṟṟiyum, iḻivu paṟṟiyumeṉa uṇarka eṉa viḷakkuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
innūṟpāviṉ poruḷiyum, `itaṉkaṇ palavayiṉāṉum eṉṟa collāṟṟal koṇṭu uriyāciriyarkaḷ uyttuṇarntu kūṟiyataṉiyum tokuttu nōkki, avi aṅṅaṉam oru muṭipu kōṭaṟkuc ciṟappum mikutiyum iḻipum kāraṇamātali viḷakki,
	377.	tiṇi pāl poruḷpala viraviṉa ciṟappiṉum

pālar pārppār eṉpāri

		`mūrkkaṉum mutaliyuṅ koṇṭatu viṭā
eṉa iḻipiṉāṉaḵṟiṇi muṭipēṟṟaṉa.
		`tollināl vakittōḻarun tūmaṇi neṭuntēr
mallaṟ ṟampiyu māmaṉu matuviri kamaḻtārc
celvaṉtātiyuñ ceḻunaka roṭuvaḷanāṭum
valli tokkatu vaḷaṅkeḻu kōyilu ḷoruṅkē.
