uṇmi, iṉmi mutaliya paṇpiṉi yaṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭuvarum akaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ `muṉṉaravvoṭu varuvi avvum eṉpavaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkutalāl, uḷḷa eṉpatu mutalākac cūttirattuḷ eṭuttōtappaṭṭa eṭṭup peyarkaḷum kuṟippu viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarkaḷ eṉak koḷvar civañāṉamuṉivar.
iṉṉavum eṉṟamiyāl, yā, piṟa, maṟṟiya eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu aḵṟiṇip paṉmipporuḷ kuṟittu varuvaṉa vellām koḷḷappaṭum.
	171.	terinili yuṭiya vaḵṟiṇi yiyaṟpeyar
orumiyum paṉmiyum viṉiyoṭu variṉē.
itu, pāl viḷaṅkāta aḵṟiṇi yiyaṟpeyar pāluṇarttumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kaḷḷoṭu civaṇāta aḵṟiṇi yiyaṟpeyar ēṟṟa viṉiyoṭu toṭarntavaḻi orumiyum paṉmiyum viḷaṅku niliyuṭiyaṉa. eṉṟavāṟu.
`kaḷḷoṭu civaṇum eṉa muṟkūṟiya aṭimoḻiyiṉṟi, aḵṟiṇiyiyaṟpeyar eṉak kūṟavē, kaḷḷoṭu civaṇāta aḵṟiṇiyiyaṟpeyar eṉpatu pulaṉām. aḵṟiṇiyiyaṟ peyar viṉiyoṭuvariṉ orumiyum paṉmiyum terinili yuṭiya eṉa iyiyum. terinili - teriyum nili; pālviḷaṅkum nili.
(u-m)
ā vantatu, ā vantaṉa;
yāṉi vantatu, yāṉi vantaṉa;
maram vaḷarntatu, maram vaḷarntaṉa.
eṉa ēṟṟa viṉiyāl orumiyum paṉmiyum viḷaṅkiyavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. viṉiyoṭuvariṉ eṉṟārāyiṉum ā oṉṟu, ā pala eṉa muṭikkuñ collākiya peyarāṟ pālaṟiyap paṭutalum koḷka.
ivvāṟu orumiyum paṉmiyum pakutturittaṟkiyalāta vāṟu aminta aḵṟiṇiyiyaṟpeyarkaḷ orumi viṉiyum paṉmi viṉiyum koṇṭu muṭitaṟkēṟṟavāṟu aḵṟiṇiyiru pāṟkum potuvāy niṟṟali,
	280.	pālpakā aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷ pāṟpotumiya.
eṉavarum cūttirattāl uṇarttuvar naṉṉūlār.
aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷuḷ mēṟ pālpakutturittavi yoḻittu oḻinta iyaṟpeyarkaḷaṉittum orumi paṉmi kaṭkup potuvāy niṟpaṉavām eṉpatu itaṉporuḷ.
	172.	irutiṇic coṟkum ōraṉṉa vurimiyiṟ
ṟiripuvēṟu paṭūum ellāp peyarum
niṉiyuṅ kālit tatta marapiṉ
viṉiyō ṭallatu pāṟeri pilavē.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē viravuppeyarāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) irutiṇic collātaṟkum otta urimiya vātaliṉ uyartiṇikkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi uyartiṇip peyarāyum aḵṟiṇikkaṭṭa ceṉṟuḻi aḵṟiṇip peyarāyum tirintu vēṟupaṭum viravup peyarellām, ārāyuṅkālattu avvat tiṇiyi yuṇarttutaṟkuriya muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya viṉic collōṭu iyintallatu tiṇi viḷaṅka nillā. eṉṟavāṟu.
tattamarapiṉ viṉiyāvaṉa, uyartiṇikkum aḵṟiṇikku muriya patiṉōrīṟṟup paṭarkkiviṉi.
(u-m) cāttaṉ vantāṉ, cāttaṉ vantatu; cātti vantāḷ, cātti vantatu - eṉavarum. ciṟappuṭip poruḷit tāṉiṉitu kiḷattal eṉpataṉāl viṉiyōṭallatu pāl teripila eṉṟārēṉum cāttaṉ oruvaṉ, cāttaṉ oṉṟu eṉat tattamarapiṟ peyaroṭu vantu pāl viḷakkuluṅ koḷka.
iru tiṇikkum potuvākiya col viṉiyāṟ potumi nīṅki orutiṇic collām eṉpatu karuttākaliṉ, īṇṭup pāl eṉṟatu tiṇiyiṉi.
	173.	nikaḻūu niṉṟa palarvari kiḷaviyiṉ
uyartiṇip yorumi tōṉṟalu murittē
aṉṉa marapiṉ viṉivayi ṉāṉa.
itu, viravuppeyar tattamarapiṉ viṉiyallāta viravu viṉiyālum tiṇiyaṟiyappaṭum eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nikaḻkālam paṟṟivarum palar varikiḷaviyāl uyartiṇi yorumippāl tōṉṟutalum urittu; av vorumippāl tōṉṟutaṟkēṟṟa viṉiyiṉkaṇ. eṉṟavāṟu.
`niḻakaḻūu niṉṟa palarvarikiḷavi eṉṟatu, paṭarkki yiṭattup palarpāli nīkki ēṉi nāṉku pāṟkum potuvāy nikaḻkālam uṇattivarum ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟiṉi. pallōr paṭarkki, muṉṉili taṉmi, yavvayiṉ mūṉṟum nikaḻuṅ kālattuc, ceyyumeṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyoṭu koḷḷā (tol- viṉiyiyal - 30) eṉavarum viṉiyiyaṟ cūttirattu `nikaḻuṅ kālattuc ceyyumeṉkiḷavi eṉṟatum atu.
(u-m) cāttaṉ yāḻeḻūum, cātti cāntarikkum, eṉṟavaḻi cāttaṉ cātti eṉṉum potuppeyarum, yāḻ eḻūutalum cāntarittalum ākiya viṉiyum, collamip pāl iru tiṇikkum potuvāyiṉum, muṟiyē yāḻ vācittal cantaṉatti arittal eṉṉum ciṟappu viṉiyākiya poruḷamippāl uyartiṇi āṇum peṇṇumākiya orumippāl viḷaṅkiyavāṟu kāṇka. `tōṉṟalum urittē eṉṉum etirmaṟiyummiyāl, palarvari kiḷaviyāl uyar tiṇiyorumi tōṉṟāmiyum urittu eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. irutiṇikkum potuvākiya naṭattal, kiṭattal mutalākiya potuviṉi paṟṟivarum palarvari kiḷaviyākiya ceyyumeṉ muṟṟāl uyartiṇi yorumi tōṉṟāmiyiṉ `aṉṉa marapiṉ viṉivayiṉāṉ eṉa atu tōṉṟumiṭam ituveṉa varintōtiṉār.
iṉi, uyartiṇiyorumi tōṉṟalum urittē eṉṟa ummiyāl, aḵṟiṇiyorumi tōṉṟalum urittu eṉap poruḷ kūṟi, cāttaṉ pulmēyum, cātti pulmēyum eṉṟa vaḻi aḵṟiṇi eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu eṉpar teyvacciliyār.
	174.	iyaṟpeyar ciṉippeyar ciṉimutaṟ peyarē
muṟippeyark kiḷavi tāmē tāṉē
yellā nīyir nīyeṉak kiḷantu
colliya valla piṟavu māaṅ
kaṉṉavi tōṉṟi ṉavaṟṟoṭuṅ koḷalē.
iḵtu irutiṇikkumuriya viravuppeyarkaḷ iviyeṉat tokuttuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) iyaṟpeyar mutalāka nī eṉpatīṟāka eṭuttōtap paṭṭaṉavum aviyallāta attaṉmiya piṟavum avviru tiṇip poruṇmiyiṉum viravit tōṉṟumāyiṉ av virutiṇikku muriya viravup peyarākak koḷka. eṉṟavāṟu.
iyaṟpeyar - cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu peyarāka vaḻaṅkutaṟkuriyaṉavāyk kāraṇamiṉṟip poruḷē paṟṟivarum iṭukuṟip peyar.
ciṉippeyar - peruṅkālaṉ, muṭavaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu ciṉiyuṭimiyākiya kāraṇampaṟṟi mutaṟporuḷmēl vaḻaṅkum peyar.
ciṉimutaṟpeyar cīttaliccāttaṉ, koṭumpuṟa maruti eṉṟāṟpōṉṟu ciṉiyoṭu toṭarntu varum mutaṟ poruḷiṉ peyar.
muṟippeyarkkiḷavi - tanti, tāy eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟu muṟipaṟṟi ammuṟiyuṭiya poruḷmēl varum peyar. iṅku muṟi eṉṟatu piṟappiṉāl oruvarōṭu oruvark kuḷatākiya iyipiṉi.
tām, tāṉ, ellām, nīyir, nī eṉṟa peyar aintum tammi yuṇartti niṉṟaṉa.
piṟavum eṉṟataṉāl, maka, kuḻavi pōlvaṉa irutiṇikku muriya viravup peyarākak koḷḷappaṭum. viravup peyarāka iṅku eṇṇappaṭṭa ivi oru kāraṇattāl irutiṇip poruḷum uṇarttuvaṉavātalāl, poruḷ tōṟum vēṟu vēṟu kāraṇam paṟṟi vaḻaṅkum palporuḷoru colliṉ vēṟāyiṉa eṉpar cēṉāvariyar.
	175.	avaṟṟuḷ,
nāṉkē yiyaṟpeyar nāṉkē ciṉippeyar
nāṉkeṉa moḻimaṉār ciṉimutaṟ peyarē
muṟip peyarkkiḷavi yiraṇṭā kummē
ēṉip peyarē tatta marapiṉa.
itu, mēl tokutturikkappaṭṭa viravuppeyarkaḷi vakuttu virikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟcollappaṭṭa viravuppeyaruḷ iyaṟpeyar nāṉku vakippaṭum; ciṉippeyar nāṉku vakippaṭum; ciṉimutaṟpeyar nāṉku vakippaṭum; muṟippeyar iraṇṭu vakippaṭum ēṉiya tām, tāṉ, ellām, nīyir, nī eṉa ōtiya aintum tattam ilakkaṇattaṉa. eṉṟavāṟu.
tattam marapiṉa eṉṟatu, palapeyarkaḷik kuṟitta tokutip peyarākātu ōroṉṟāy niṉṟa tammikkuṟittu vaḻaṅkum taṉippeyarkaḷām eṉṟavāṟu. tokutippeyar patiṉāṉkum taṉippeyar aintumāka viravuppeyar pattoṉpatu eṉpatām.
	176.	avitām,
peṇmi yiyaṟpeya rāṇmi yiyaṟpeyar
paṉmi yiyaṟpeya rorumi yiyaṟpeyareṉ
ṟannāṉ keṉpa viyaṟpeyar niliyē.
iḵtu iyaṟpeyar nāṉkum iviyeṉa virittuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟcollappaṭṭa iyaṟpeyartām irutiṇik kaṇṇum peṇmip poruṇmiyik kuṟitta iyaṟ peyarum, āṇmip poruṇmiyik kuṟitta iyaṟpeyarum, paṉmip poruṇmiyik kuṟitta iyaṟpeyarum, orumip poruṇmiyik kuṟitta iyaṟpeyarum eṉa avaṟṟatu nilimi annāṉkām eṉpar āciriyar. eṉṟavāṟu.
`paṉmi iyaṟpeyar eṉṟārāyiṉum uyartiṇip paṉmi iyaṟpeyar, pāltōṉṟaliṉ aḵtoḻittu ēṉiyatu koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar teyvacciliyār.
(u-m) cātti, cāttaṉ, yāṉi, kōti eṉavarum.
	177.	peṇmic ciṉippeyar āṇmic ciṉippeyar
paṉmic ciṉippeyar orumic ciṉip peyareṉ
ṟannāṉ keṉpa ciṉippeyar niliyē.
itu, ciṉippeyar nāṉkum iviyeṉa uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟcollappaṭṭa ciṉippeyaratu nilimi yāvatu, peṇmip poruṇmik kaṇvarum ciṉippeyar, āṇmip poruṇmikkaṇ varum ciṉippeyar, paṉmip poruṇmikkaṇ varum ciṉippeyar, orumip poruṇmik kaṇ varum ciṉippeyar eṉa annāṉkām. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) muṭatti, muṭavaṉ, neṭuṅkaḻuttal, kaṇṇili eṉavarum. muṭam, kaḻuttiṉ nīṭci, kaṇṇiṉ kuruṭṭut taṉmi mutaliyaṉa ciṉiyatu vikāramātaliṉ ciṉiyāyiṉa.
	178.	peṇmi cuṭṭiya ciṉimutaṟ peyarē
āṇmi cuṭṭiya ciṉimutaṟ peyarē
paṉmi cuṭṭiya ciṉimutaṟ peyarē
orumi cuṭṭiya ciṉimutaṟ peyareṉ
ṟannāṉ keṉpa ciṉimutaṟ peyarē.
itu, ciṉimutaṟ peyar nāṉkum iviyeṉa uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) ciṉimutaṟ peyaratu nilimi peṇmip poruṇmiyic cuṭṭiya ciṉimutaṟpeyarum āṇmip poruṇmiyic cuṭṭiya ciṉimutaṟ peyarum paṉmip poruṇmiyic cuṭṭiya ciṉimutaṟ peyarum orumip poruṇmiyic cuṭṭiya ciṉimutaṟ peyarum eṉa annāṉkām eṉpar āciriyar. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) muṭakkoṟṟi, muṭakkoṟṟaṉ, peruṅkāl  yāṉi, koṭumpuṟamaruti eṉavarum.
	179.	peṇmi muṟippeyar āṇmi muṟippeyareṉ
ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa muṟippeyar niliyē.
itu, muṟippeyar iraṇṭum iviyeṉa uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) peṇmip poruṇmiyic cuṭṭiya muṟip peyarum āṇmip poruṇmiyic cuṭṭiya muṟip peyarum eṉa muṟippeyaratu nilimi iraṇṭākum. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) tāy, tanti eṉavarum.
iṅṅaṉam āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār āṟu cūttiraṅ kaḷāl tokuttum vakuttum uṇarttiya irutiṇikkum uriya potup peyarkaḷōṭu, avar kūṟāta taṉmip peyarkaḷiyum muṉṉiliyil avar kūṟāta ellīr nīvir nīr eṉpavaṟṟiyum irutiṇikkum uriya potup peyarākak koṇṭu,
	281.	mutaṟpeyar nāṉkuñ ciṉippeyar nāṉkum
ciṉimutaṟ peyaroṭu nāṉkum muṟiyiraṇṭum
taṉmi nāṉkum muṉṉili aintum
ellām tāmtāṉ iṉṉaṉa potuppeyar.
eṉavum,
	282.	āṇmi peṇmi orumi paṉmiyiṉ
āman nāṉmikaḷ; āṇpeṇ muṟippeyar.
eṉavum varum iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷāl tokuttum vakuttum uṇarttiṉār pavaṇanti muṉivar.
iṅku mutaṟpeyar eṉṟatu iyaṟpeyari.
mutaṟpeyar nāṉkum, ciṉippeyar nāṉkum, ciṉi mutaṟpeyar nāṉkum, muṟippeyar iraṇṭum, yāṉ, nāṉ, yām, nām eṉṉum taṉmip peyar nāṉkum, ellīr, nīyir, nīvir, nīr, nī eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyar aintum, ellām, tām, tāṉ eṉavarum oṉpatuvakip peyarkaḷum irutiṇikkum potuvāṉa peyarkaḷākum. ivaṟṟuḷ mutaṟ peyar, ciṉip peyar, ciṉimutaṟpeyar eṉpaṉa āṇmi, peṇmi, orumi, paṉmi kāraṇamāka ovvoṉṟum nannāṉkākum. muṟippeyar āṇ peṇ eṉa iraṇṭākum eṉpatu in naṉṉūṟ cūttiraṅkaḷiṉ poruḷākum.
	180.	peṇmi cuṭṭiya ellāp peyarum
oṉṟaṟkum oruttikkum oṉṟiya niliyē.
itu, peṇmi cuṭṭiya peyar irutiṇikkaṇṇum taṉakkuriya pāluṇarttumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) peṇmi cuṭṭivarum nāṉku peyarum aḵṟiṇip peṇṇākiya oṉṟaṟkum uyartiṇi oruttikkum otta nilimiya. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) cātti vantatu, cātti  vantāḷ eṉavum, muṭatti vantatu, muṭatti vantāḷ eṉavum, muṭakkoṟṟi vantatu, muṭakkoṟṟi vantāḷ eṉavum tāy vantatu, tāy vantāḷ eṉavum muṟiyē iyaṟpeyar, ciṉippeyar, ciṉimutaṟ peyar, muṟippeyar ākiya nāṉkum aḵṟiṇip peṇṇorumikkum uyartiṇip peṇmikkum uriyavāy vantavāṟu kaṇṭu koḷka.
oṉṟaṟkum eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉārēṉum peṇmi cuṭṭiya peyareṉṟamiyāl aḵṟiṇip peṇṇākiya oṉṟiyē kuṟikkum. ivvāṟē āṇmi cuṭṭiya ellāp peyarum eṉa aṭuttu varum cūttirattum oṉṟu eṉpatu aḵṟiṇiyāṇākiya oṉṟiyē kuṟikkumeṉak koḷka. oṉṟiya nili - ottanili. oṉṟiya niliyuṭiyavaṟṟi oṉṟiya nili eṉṟār.
	181.	āṇmi cuṭṭiya vellāp peyarum
oṉṟaṟkum oruvaṟkum oṉṟiya niliyē.
iḵtu, āṇmi cuṭṭiya peyar irutiṇikkaṇṇum taṉakkuriya pāl uṇarttumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) āṇmi cuṭṭivarum nāṉkupeyarum aḵṟiṇi āṇākiya oṉṟaṟkum uyartiṇi oruvaṉukkum otta nili miya. eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
cāttaṉ vantatu, cāttaṉ vantāṉ eṉavum,
muṭavaṉ vantatu, muṭavaṉ vantāṉ eṉavum,
muṭak koṟṟaṉ vantatu, muṭakkoṟṟaṉ vantāṉ eṉavum,
tanti vantatu, tanti vantāṉ eṉavum
muṟiyē iyaṟpeyar, ciṉippeyar, ciṉimutaṟpeyar, muṟippeyar ākiya nāṉkum aḵṟiṇi āṇorumikkum uyartiṇiyāṇmikkum uriyavāy vantami kāṇka.
	182.	paṉmi cuṭṭiya ellāp peyarum
oṉṟē palavē oruvar eṉṉum
eṉṟip pāṟkum ōraṉ ṉavvē.
itu, paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar irutiṇikkaṇṇum pāluṇarttumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) paṉmi cuṭṭivarum iyaṟpeyar, ciṉippeyar, ciṉi mutaṟpeyar eṉṉum mūṉṟu peyarum aḵṟiṇi yorumiyum attiṇip paṉmiyum uyartiṇi āṇ peṇ eṉṉum orumiyum ākiya ippālkaḷukkum otta nili mi. eṉṟavāṟu.
oṉṟu eṉṟatu, aḵṟiṇi āṇorumi, peṇṇo rumiyiṉi, oruvar eṉṟatu uyartiṇi āṇorumi, peṇṇorumiyiṉi.
(u-m) yāṉi vantatu, yāṉi vantaṉa, yāṉivantāṉ, yāṉi vantāḷ eṉavum, neṭuṅkaḻuttal vantatu, neṭuṅkaḻuttal vantaṉa, neṭuṅkaḻuttal vantāṉ, neṭuṅkaḻuttal vantāḷ eṉavum, peruṅkāl yāṉi vantatu, peruṅkāl yāṉi vantaṉa, peruṅkāl yāṉi vantāṉ, peruṅkāl yāṉi vantāḷ eṉavum muṟiyē paṉmi iyaṟpeyar, paṉmic ciṉippeyar, paṉmic ciṉimutaṟpeyar mūṉṟum aḵṟiṇi yorumikkum at tiṇip paṉmikkum uyartiṇi āṇorumi peṇṇoru mikkum uriyavāy vantami kāṇka.
paṉmikkēyaṉṟi orumikkum uriyavāy varuvaṉa vaṟṟip paṉmip peyareṉṟatu eṉṉi? eṉa viṉā nikaḻttik koṇṭu, paṉmip peyar orumiyuṇarttu māyiṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉ uṇarttappaṭāta paṉmiyi orukāl uṇarttaliṉ appaṉmi yāṉ avi varintu cuṭṭappaṭutaliṉ appeyara vāyiṉa eṉa viṭi kūṟuvar cēṉāvariyar.
aṭimoḻikaḷ iṉamuḷḷatum iṉamillatum eṉa iru vakippaṭum. centāmari eṉpuḻic cemmiyākiya aṭi moḻi attāmarikku iṉamākiya veṇṭāmariyākiya iṉamuṇmi cuṭṭi ataṉōṭuḷatākiya iyipiṉi nīkki niṟṟaliṉ itu piṟitiṉiyipu nīkkiya vicēṭaṇam eṉappaṭum. ceññāyiṟu eṉpuḻic cemmiyākiya aṭimoḻi karumi mutaliya piṟa niṟamuṭiya piṟa ñāyiṟuṇṭeṉa iṉañ cuṭṭātu, cemmi niṟam ñāyiṟṟōṭu iyipuṭiyatu eṉṟa aḷavil ñāyiṟṟiyē cuṭṭi niṟṟaliṉ, iṉamillā aṭimoḻiyākiya itu taṉṉōṭiyi piṉmi nīkkiya vicēṭaṇam eṉappaṭum.
paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyareṉpatu, `veṇkuṭip peruviṟal eṉpatu pōla, orumiyiyipu nīkkātu iyipiṉmi māttiri nīkkip paṉmi cuṭṭum eṉpatu paṭa niṉṟatu eṉpar cēṉā variyar. iccūttirattiṟ kūṟiyavāṟu paṉmi cuṭṭiyapeyar uyartiṇip paṉmiyi yuṇarttā toḻitalum ēṉi yorumikaḷi yuṇarttutalum poruntā veṉpatu karuti it tolkāppiyac cūttirak karutti maṟuttal eṉṉum matampaṭa `avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟēyirutiṇit taṉ pāl ēṟkum (naṉ- 283) eṉap pavaṇantiyār cūttirañ ceytāreṉpar caṅkara namaccivāyar. iṉi, paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar eṉpataṟku irutiṇiyilum paṉmip pālic cuṭṭivarum potup peyar eṉpatē poruḷākum. ituvē tolkāppiyaṉār karutteṉpatu, tāmeṉkiḷavi paṉmik kurittē (tol-col- 181) eṉavum ēṉikkiḷavi paṉmik kurittē (tol-col- 187) eṉavum varuñ cūttiraṅkaḷil uyartiṇip palar pāṟkum aḵṟiṇip palaviṉ pāṟkum potuvākiya niliyiṉip `paṉmi eṉṟa collāl āciriyar cuṭṭutalāl naṉku pulaṉām. iṅṅaṉam paṉmi cuṭṭiya potuppeyarkaḷ tamakkuriya irutiṇip paṉmiyiyuñ cuṭṭi vaḻaṅkutalē muṟiyākavum, avaṟṟuḷ orucār peyarkaḷ, aḵṟiṇiyil oṉṟaṉpāl palaviṉpāl uyartiṇiyil āṇorumi peṇṇorumi ākiya innāṉku pālkaḷiyum kuṟittu varutaluṇṭeṉpār, `oṉṟē palavē oruvar eṉṉum, eṉṟippāṟkum ōraṉṉavvē eṉac cūttirañ ceytār tolkāppiyaṉār. itaṉkaṇ `eṉṟip pāṟkum eṉṟa eccavummiyāl paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar taṉakkuriya irutiṇip paṉmiyiyum ēṟṟu varutali āciriyar taḻīik kūṟiṉārātal vēṇṭum. ivvuṇmi, taṉpālēṟṟali ummiyāṟ ṟaḻīiyiṉār eṉavarum civañāṉa muṉivar urik kuṟippiṉāl iṉitu pulaṉātal kāṇka.
(u-m) tām vantār, tām vantaṉa eṉap paṉmip potup peyar uyartiṇip paṉmiyum aḵṟiṇip paṉmiyum uṇartti niṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, uyartiṇi āṇorumi, peṇṇorumi aḵṟiṇi orumi paṉmi eṉap pala pālkaḷiyum cuṭṭi niṟṟaliṟ paṉmi cuṭṭiyapeyar eṉṟum, āṇmi cuṭṭiya peyar, peṇmi cuṭṭiya peyar, orumi cuṭṭiyapeyar eṉpaṉavum pala pālkaḷic cuṭṭi niṉṟaṉavāyiṉum, iraṇṭu pālkaḷiyum mūṉṟu pālkaḷiyum cuṭṭi niṟkum appeyarkaḷ, oruvaṉ, orutti, oṉṟu, pala eṉṉum nāṉku pālkaḷiyum cuṭṭi niṟkum ivaṟṟi nōkkac ciṉmi cuṭṭiya peyareṉap paṭuvatallatu paṉmi kaṭṭiya peyarākā eṉṟum, aṉṟiyum uyartiṇi āṇorumi peṇṇorumi aḵṟiṇi yorumi eṉṟa mūṉṟum orumippāl eṉa oṉṟāyaṭaṅkutaliṉ avi palapālkaḷiyum cuṭṭi niṉṟaṉa alla ākalāṉ ammūṉṟoru miyuñ cuṭṭiya peyarkaḷ orumi cuṭṭiya peyareṉa aṭaṅkumeṉṟum, iḵtu orumiyum paṉmiyu meṉṉum iru pālkaḷiyum cuṭṭi niṟṟalāṟ paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyarāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum evvakiyāl nōkkiṉum paṉmi cuṭṭiya eṉṉum ivvaṭimoḻi iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻiyē eṉṟum itaṉ peyark kāraṇatti viḷakkuvar civañāṉamuṉivar.

		 kaḻaṉi nallūr makiḻnark keṉ
iḻi nekiḻ paruvaral ceppātōyē
eṉavum villāṉ, toṭiyāḷ, nallār, muṉṉiyār, ceppātāy eṉavarum coṟkaḷil āṉ, āḷ, ār, āy eṉavarum vikuti kaḷiluḷḷa ākāram ceyyuḷil ōkāramāyt tirintavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
cēramāṉ, maliyamāṉ, eṉṉum peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa vikutiyiṉ ākāram ivvāṟu tiriyāmiyiṉ āyiṭaṉ aṟital eṉṟār. `āvōvākum peyarum eṉṉam eccavummiyāl akaram ōkāramākat tiriyum peyarum uḷa eṉak kūṟik kiḻavaṉ kiḻavaṉ eṉpaṉa nāṭu kiḻavōṉ, kiḻavōṭēttu eṉavum varum eṉa utāraṇaṅkāṭṭuvar teyvac ciliyār.
ivvāṟu peyar viṉikaḷiṉ īṟṟil niṉṟa āṉ, āḷ, ār, āy eṉṉum vikutikaḷiṉ īṟṟayal ākāram ceyyuḷil ōkāramākat tiriyavumpeṟum eṉpataṉi,
	352.	peyarviṉi yiṭattu ṉaḷaraya vīṟṟayal
āvō vākaluñ ceyyuḷuḷurittē.
eṉavarum cūttirattāl uṇarttiṉār naṉṉūlār.
	196.	iṟiccip poruḷvayiṟ ceyyuḷuṭ kiḷakkum
iyaṟpeyark kiḷavi yuyartiṇi cuṭṭā
nilattuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟa lāṉa.
iḵtu orucār iyaṟpeyark kuriyatōr marapuṇarttukiṉṟatu. eytiyatu vilakkiyatumām.
(i-ḷ) ceyyuḷuṭ karupporuḷmēṟ kiḷakkappaṭum irutiṇikkumuriya iyaṟpeyar uyartiṇiyuṇarttā; avvan nilattuvaḻi aḵṟiṇip poruḷavāy vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varutalāl. eṉṟavāṟu.
iṟiccip poruḷ eṉpatu ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum akaṉintiṇikkuriya māvum puḷḷu mutalākiya karupporuḷ. iyaṟpeyark kiḷavi eṉṟatu, akkarup poruḷkaḷik kuṟittu iṭukuṟiyāki vaḻaṅkum peyari. nilamāvaṉa mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal eṉpaṉa. iyaṟpeyar eṉpataṉi irutiṇikkum potuveṉa ōtiṉārāyiṉum ceyyuḷakattuk karupporuḷāki mulli mutaliya nilattuvaḻit toṉṟum māvum puḷḷum maramum mutalāyiṉavaṟṟiṉmēl iṭukuṟiyākivarum iyaṟpeyar aḵṟiṇip poruḷic cuṭṭuvatallatu uyartiṇip poruṇmiyic cuṭṭamāṭṭā eṉa eytiyatu vilakkiyavāṟu.
(u-m)
		`kaṭuvaṉ mutumakaṉ kallā mūlaṟku
vatuvi yayarnta vaṉpaṟaḻk kumari
eṉpuḻi, kaṭuvaṉ, mūlaṉ, kumari eṉavarum viravuppeyarākiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ aḵṟiṇip poruḷavāyallatu annilattu vaḻi vaḻaṅkāmiyiṉ avi uyartiṇi cuṭṭāmi kaṇṭu koḷka. kaṭuvaṉ, mūlaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷil āṇ pāluṇarttum aṉṉīṟum kumari eṉpatil peṇpāluṇarttum ikaravīṟum amintiruttalāṉum iṅṅaṉam āṇiyum peṇṇiyum varintuṇarttum taṉmi aḵṟiṇik kiṉmi yāṉum ivi aḵṟiṇip peyarākātu irutiṇikkumuriya potup peyarātal pulaṉām. alavaṉ, kaḷvaṉ eṉpaṉa ṉakaravīṟṟaṉavāyiṉum avi āṇpāluṇarttātu naṇṭu eṉṉum iṉattiyuṇartti niṟṟaliṉ aḵṟiṇip peyarāvataṉṟi viravuppeyarākāmi yuṇarka.
	197.	tiṇiyoṭu paḻakiya peyaralaṅ kaṭiyē.
iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) karupporuḷuṇarttum viravuppeyar uyartiṇi cuṭṭātu aḵṟiṇiyic cuṭṭutal avvattiṇikkuriyavāy vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuvarum peyarallātaviṭattu. eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē tiṇiyoṭu paḻaki vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varum viravuppeyar irutiṇiyuñ cuṭṭivarum eṉpatām.
(u-m) cerumiku muṉpiṟ kūrvēṟ kāḷi eṉavum tiruntu vēl viṭaliyoṭu varumeṉatāyēeṉavum uyartiṇi cuṭṭivantavāṟu kāṇka. kāḷi viṭali eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉa poruḷ vakiyāṉaṉṟip pāli mutaliya nilattukka urimipūṇṭu niṟṟaliṉ tiṇiyoṭu paḻakiya peyareṉap paṭṭaṉa.

6. viṉiyiyal
viṉiyeṉpatu palaporu ḷorucollāyt toḻiṟ paṇpiṉiyum ataṉ kāriyamākiya viṉi nikaḻcciyiyum uṇarttum. toḻiṟpaṇpi uṇarttum colli uric colleṉavum ataṉ kāriyamākiya viṉinikaḻcciyi yuṇarttuñ colli viṉic colleṉavum kūṟutal marapu. viṉic collāvatu vēṟṟumiyurupu ēlātu veḷippaṭi yākavum kuṟippākavum kālamuṇartti niṟpatākum. iṟantakālam, nikaḻkālam, etirkālam eṉak kālam mūṉṟām. iṟantakālam eṉpatu toḻil muṟṟuppeṟṟa nili. nikaḻkālam eṉpatu toḻil toṭaṅki muṭivupeṟātu toṭarntu nikaḻum nili. etirkālam eṉpatu toḻilē toṭaṅkap peṟāta nili. immūvakik kālaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟi veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭuñ coṟkaḷi viṉi eṉṟum, ikkālaṅkaḷik kuṟippāka uṇarttuñ coṟkaḷik kuṟippu eṉṟum kūṟuvar tolkāppiyar. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē teriniliviṉiyeṉṟum kuṟippuviṉi yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar piṟkāla āciriyarkaḷ. iv viṉic coṟkaḷ muṟṟu, viṉiyeccam, peyareccam eṉa mūvakippaṭum. pāluṇarttum īṟukaḷākiya vikutikaḷōṭu kūṭipporuḷ muṟṟi (niṟintu) niṟkuñ coṟkaḷ viṉimuṟṟuk kaḷākum. aimpālākiya viṉi mutali yuṇarttum vikutiyuṟuppuk kuṟinta kuṟiccoṟkaḷāy maṟṟoru viṉic col lōṭallatu muṟṟuppeṟātu niṟpaṉa viṉiyeccaṅ kaḷākum. pāluṇarttum vikutiyiṉṟik kuṟinta kuṟic coṟkaḷāyp peyari eccamākavuṭiya viṉic coṟkaḷ peyareccam eṉappaṭum. immūvaki viṉiccoṟkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamuṇarttiṉamiyāl itu viṉiyiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
ivviyaliluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷi 49-āka iḷampūraṇarum 51-ākac cēṉāvariyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum 54-ākat teyvac ciliyārum pakuttu uri varintuḷḷārkaḷ. eccaviyaliluḷḷa `iṟappiṉ nikaḻviṉ `evvayiṉviṉiyum, `avitām, tattaṅkiḷavi, eṉavarum mūṉṟu cūttiraṅkaḷiyum viṉiyilak kaṇamāta loppumi paṟṟi ivviyaliṉ iṟutiyil teyvac ciliyār cērtturittamiyāl avar karuttuppaṭi ivviyaliṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ 54- āyiṉa. viṉic coṟkaḷ ellā vaṟṟiyum uyartiṇik kuriyaṉa, aḵṟiṇik kuriyaṉa, irutiṇikkumuriyaṉa eṉa mūṉṟu vakiyākat tolkāppiyar ivviyaliṟ pakuttuṇarttukiṉṟār.
	198.	viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu
niṉiyuṅ kālik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum
itu, viṉic coṟkellām potuvilakkaṇam uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) viṉiyeṉṟu collappaṭuvatu vēṟṟumiyoṭu poruntātu ārāyuṅkāṟ kālattoṭu pulappaṭum. eṉṟavāṟu.
iṅku vēṟṟumi eṉṟatu, vēṟṟumiyurupiṉi.
(u-m) uṇṭāṉ, kariyaṉ eṉa vēṟṟumi koḷātu kālamoṭu tōṉṟiyavāṟu kaṇṭu koḷka. `vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu eṉṉātu `kālamoṭu tōṉṟum eṉpatē kūṟiṉ, viṉiyālaṇiyum peyarum toḻiṟpeyarum viṉic col eṉak koḷḷanērum. `kālamoṭu tōṉṟum eṉṉātu `vēṟṟumikoḷḷātu eṉṟu maṭṭum kūṟiṉ, iṭic collum uriccollum viṉiccol eṉak koḷḷa vēṇṭi varum. ātalāl ivvirutiṟamum nīkkutaṟku vēṟṟumi koḷātu kālamoṭu tōṉṟum eṉṟār. viṉic colluḷ veḷippaṭak kālam viḷakkātaṉavum uḷa. aviyum ārāyuṅkāl kālamuṭiyaṉa eṉṟaṟku niṉiyuṅkāli eṉṟār. ivvāṟu piṉṉuṇarttappaṭum viṉiccoṟkellām potuvilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉār eṉpar cēṉāvariyar.
viṉiyeṉṟatu, uṇ, tiṉ, karu, cey eṉavarum mutaṉiliyi eṉavum, iḵtu ākupeyarāyt taṉṉāṟ piṟakkiṉṟa colliyuṇarttiṟṟu eṉavum, uṇṭal, tiṉṟal, karumi, cemmi eṉpaṉa ammutaṉiliyāṟ piṟanta viṉippeyar eṉavum, uṇṭāṉ, tiṉṟāṉ, kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ eṉpaṉa am mutaṉiliyiṟ piṟanta viṉiccol eṉavum kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
viṉi eṉpatu toḻil uṇarttum collātaliṉ atu vēṟṟumi koṇṭu niṟpatum orunili uṇṭu. annili yoḻiyak kālattōṭu porunti niṟkumatu nammāl viṉic col eṉa vēṇṭappaṭṭatu eṉpatu iccūttirattiṉ karuttāka viḷakkuvar teyvacciliyār.
iṅṅaṉam vēṟṟumi koḷḷāmiyum kālamoṭu tōṉṟutalum viṉiccoṟkē yilakkaṇamātali naṉṉūlār `toḻilala kālantōṟṟā, vēṟṟumik kiṭaṉāy (naṉṉūl - 274) eṉap peyarilakkaṇam uṇarttumvaḻi etirmaṟi mukattāl uṇarak kūṟiyuḷḷami iṅku niṉikkat takuvatākum.
viṉic collāvatu ituveṉavuṇarttuvatu,
	319.	ceypavaṉ karuvi nilañceyal kālañ
ceyporu ḷāṟun taruvatu viṉiyē.
eṉavarum naṉṉūṟ cūttiramākum.
karuttāvum karuviyum nilaṉum toḻilum kālamum ceyappaṭuporuḷum eṉṉum āṟuporuḷiyum taruvatu viṉic collākum eṉpatu itaṉporuḷ.
viṉi eṉṟatu, ākupeyarāy viṉiccolli yuṇarttiṟṟu. ceypavaṉ - karuttā; orupālamēl vittōti ṉārēṉum aimpālum koḷka. iyaṟṟutaṟkaruttā, ēvutaṟ karuttā eṉṉum iraṇṭaṉiyum ceypavaṉ eṉṟum, mutaṟ kāraṇam, tuṇik kāraṇam eṉṉum iraṇṭaṉiyum karuvi eṉṟum potuppaṭak kūṟiṉār.
(u-m) vaṉintāṉ eṉṟavaḻik kulālaṉākiya iyaṟṟutaṟ karuttāvum, maṇṇākiya mutaṟkāraṇamum, taṇṭacakkara mutaliya tuṇikkāraṇaṅkaḷum, vaṉiyum iṭamum, vaṉitaṟkuc ceyalum, iṟantakālamum, kuṭam mutaliya ceyappaṭu poruḷum eṉa āṟum tōṉṟutal kāṇka. āṟum eṉṉum muṟṟummiyi eccamākki ittokiyiṟ cila kuṟintu varavum peṟum eṉap poruḷurippar. `koṭi yāṭiṟṟu eṉpuḻic ceyappaṭu poruḷum, `koṭituñcum eṉpuḻic ceyappaṭu poruḷōṭu karuviyum kuṟintu vantaṉa.
iṉi, `āṟum eṉṉum ummiyi uyarvu ciṟappummi yākak koṇṭu iḻintaṉa cilavuḷa; aviyum vēṇṭumēṟ koḷka eṉap poruḷurippar. vaṉintāṉ eṉpuḻik kuṭattit taṉakku vaṉintāṉ allatu piṟarkku vaṉintāṉ eṉa `iṉṉataṟku eṉpatum, aṟam mutaliya payaṉ karuti vaṉintāṉ eṉa `itupayaṉ eṉpatum tōṉṟutal koḷḷap paṭum. iṉṉataṟku, itu payaṉ eṉṉum iraṇṭum ētuviṉpāṟ paṭṭuk karuviyuḷ aṭaṅkutaliṉ iccūttirattuḷ vitantu eṇṇap paṭāvāyiṉa.
muṟṟu viṉikaḷuḷ pakutiyāṟ ceyalum, vikutiyāṟ karuttāvum, iṭinili mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ kālamum, peyarecca viṉiyeccaṅkaḷil immuṟiyē ceyalum kālamum veḷippaṭat tōṉṟuvaṉa. ēṉiyavi kuṟippiṟṟōṉṟuvaṉa. ceypavaṉ mutaliya ivi āṟuṅ kāraṇamākak kūṭuṅ kūṭṭattu ivaṟṟuḷ ceyal eṉṉum mutaṟkāraṇattiṉiṉṟum viṉiyākiya kāriyam oṉṟu nikaḻumātaliṉ, ivi āṟu kāraṇattiyum pulappaṭuttum kāriyamākiya viṉiyiṉ toḻili oṟṟumi nayaṅkaruti ataṉ colmēlēṟṟi `āṟum taruvatu viṉiyē eṉṟār. kāraṇattik kāriyam tarum eṉpatu, pukiyākiya kāriyam taṉakkuk kāraṇamākiya tīyuṇmiyik kāṭṭutal pōlvatām.
	199.	kālan tāmē mūṉṟeṉa moḻipa.
itu muṟkūṟiya kālam iṉitteṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa kālam mūṉṟeṉṟu colluvar pulavar. eṉṟavāṟu.
tām eṉpatu kaṭṭuric cuvipaṭa niṉṟatu.
	200.	iṟappi ṉikaḻvi ṉetirvi ṉeṉṟā
ammuk kālamuṅ kuṟippoṭuṅ koḷḷu
meynnili yuṭiya tōṉṟa lāṟē.
itu, muṟkuṟitta kālattiṉ vakiyum atutāṉ kuṟippiṟkum uṇṭeṉpatum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) mēṟ collappaṭṭa kālattiṉ pākupāṭākiya iṟantakālam, nikaḻkālam, etirkālam eṉṟu collappaṭum ammūṉṟu kālamum kuṟippoṭum poruntum poruḷ nilimiyiyuṭiyaṉa; viṉiccollāṉavi tōṉṟu neṟikkaṇ. eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, kālam mūṉṟāvaṉa iṟappu, nikaḻvu, etirvu eṉpatūum, veḷippaṭak kālam viḷaṅkātaṉa kuṟippuviṉi eṉpatūum peṟṟām.
(u-m) uṇṭāṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟāṉ, uṇpāṉ eṉpaṉa muṟiyē mūṉṟu kālamun tōṉṟa niṉṟaṉa. kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ eṉpaṉa viṉikkuṟippu.
iṟappāvatu, toḻilatu kaḻivu; nikaḻvāvatu toḻil toṭaṅkappaṭṭu muṟṟuppeṟāta nilimi; etirvāvatu toḻil piṟavāmi.
toḻil eṉpatu poruḷiṉatu puṭipeyarcciyākaliṉ aḵtu oru kaṇam niṟpatallatu iraṇṭu kaṇam nīṭittu nillāmiyiṉ, nikaḻcci yeṉpatoṉṟu ataṟkilli. eṉiṉum, uṇṭal tiṉṟal eṉap palatoḻiṟ ṟokutiyi oru toḻilākak koḷḷutaliṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟāṉ, vārā niṉṟāṉ eṉa nikaḻcciyum uṭittāyiṟṟu eṉpar cēṉāvariyar.
viṉikkuṟippuk kālamoṭu tōṉṟuṅkāl, paṇṭu kariyaṉ, itu poḻutu kariyaṉ eṉa iṟanta kālamum nikaḻ kālamum muṟiyē tōṉṟa varutalum, nāḷik kariyaṉām eṉa etir kālattu ākkamoṭu varutalum aṟika.
iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa kālattiṉ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum ākiyavaṟṟi,
	381.	iṟappetirvu nikaḻveṉak kālam mūṉṟē.
eṉavarum cūttirattāṟ kuṟippiṭuvar pavaṇantimuṉivar.
yātoru poruḷum, tōṉṟamaḷaviṟ ṟōṉṟi, vaḷaru maḷaviṉ vaḷarntu, mutirumaḷaviṉ mutirntu aḻiyumaḷaviṉ aḻiyumaṉṟi, uyirkaḷ vēṇṭiyavāṟu ākāmiyiṉ, avaṟṟi avvaḷaviṉ avvāṟu iyaṟṟuvatu kālam eṉṉum aruvap poruḷāmeṉavum, ataṉāl iyalum poruḷkaḷiṉ toḻil iṟantatum etirvatum nikaḻvatumātaliṉ avvāṟu iyaṟṟuṅ kālamum iṟappu, etirvu, nikaḻvu eṉ mūṉṟu kūṟṟatām eṉavum uyttuṇarnturippār, kālam iṉṟeṉṟum iraṇṭeṉṟum kūṟuvār kūṟṟi vilakkik `kālam mūṉṟē eṉat teḷinturittār.
	201.	kuṟippiṉum viṉiyiṉum neṟippaṭat tōṉṟik
kālamoṭu varūum viṉicco lellām
uyartiṇik kurimiyum aḵṟiṇik kurimiyum
āyiru tiṇikkumō raṉṉa vurimiyum
ammū vurupiṉa tōṉṟa lāṟē.
itu, viṉiccoṟkaḷatu pākupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) kuṟippup poruṇmikkaṇṇum toḻiṟ poruṇmik kaṇṇun tōṉṟik kālattoṭu varum ellā viṉiccollum, uyartiṇik kuriyaṉavum, aḵṟiṇik kuriyaṉavum avviraṇṭu tiṇikkum oppavuriyaṉavum eṉa mūṉṟu pakutiyaṉavām; avi tōṉṟum neṟikkaṇ. eṉṟavāṟu.
kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ eṉpuḻit toḻiṉmi teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkātu kuṟittuk koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ `kuṟippu eṉṟār.
(u-m) uṇṭāṉ, kariyaṉ eṉavum, ceṉṟatu, ceyyatu eṉavum, vantaṉi, veḷiyi eṉavum mūvaki viṉiyum muṟiyē vantaṉa.
kuṟippoṭuṅ koḷḷum eṉa mēlic cūttirattuk kuṟippu iyipupaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ `kuṟippiṉum viṉiyiṉum eṉṟār. piṉṉar īṟupaṟṟi uṇarttappaṭum viṉiccoṟkaḷi, iḵtu iṟanta kālattiṟ kuriyatu, itu nikaḻkālattiṟ kuriyatu, iḵtu etirkālattiṟkuriyatu eṉa vaḻakku nōkki uṇarntu koḷka eṉa aṟivuṟuttuvār, `kālamoṭu varūum viṉiccol eṉṟār. viṉiccol kālam uṇarttuṅkāl cila neṟip pāṭuṭiyaṉa eṉpatuṇarttuvār `neṟippaṭat tōṉṟi eṉṟār. neṟippāṭāvatu īṟṟumici (vikutiyiṉmēl) niṟkum eḻuttu vēṟupāṭu. avi muṟṟuvuṇarttalākāmiyiṉ tolkāp piyattil viritturikkap paṭāvāyiṉa. eṉiṉum tolkāppiya vuriyāciriyarkaḷ āṅkāṅkē yuṇarttic celvar.

(i-ḷ) ka ṭa taṟa eṉṉum nāṉku meyyiyūrntu varum kuṟṟiyalukaratti īṟākavuṭiya collum, eṉ, ēṉ, al eṉṉum īṟṟavākiya collum eṉa avvēḻum taṉ viṉiyit tāṉēyurikkum taṉmi yorumic collām. eṉṟavāṟu.
kuṟṟukaram nāṉkum, allum etirkālampaṟṟi varum. kuṟṟukaram kālaveḻuttup peṟuṅkāl ummīṟṟōṭu okkum. allīṟu pakaramum vakaramum (akiya kālaveḻuttup) peṟṟu varum. eṉ, ēṉ eṉpaṉa mūṉṟukālamum paṟṟi (kkāla veḻuttup peṟṟu) varum. kālaveḻuttu aṭuttaṟkaṇ em mīṟṟōṭu eṉṉīṟum, ēmīṟṟōṭu, ēṉīṟum okkum. āṇṭuk kūṟiya vikaṟpamellām aṟintu oṭṭik koḷka eṉa viḷakkam taruvar cēṉāvariyar.
(u-m) uṇku, uṇṭu, varutu, cēṟu eṉavum, uriñuku, tirumuku eṉavum, uṇṭaṉeṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟaṉeṉ, uṇku veṉ eṉavum, uṇṭēṉ, uṇṇāniṉṟēṉ, uṇpēṉ eṉavum uṇpal, varuval eṉavum varum.
uyartiṇikkuriya taṉittaṉmiyorumi vikutikaḷākiya ivvēḻuṭaṉ putiyaṉa pukutalāka vanta aṉvikutiyiyum kūṭṭi,
	330.	kuṭutuṟu veṉṉuṅ kuṉṟiya lukaramō

irutiṇi mukkūṟ ṟorumit taṉmi.
eṉac cūttirañ ceytār naṉṉūlār.
`ku, ṭu, tu, ṟu, al, aṉ, eṉ, ēṉ eṉṉum ivi eṭṭiṉiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya coṟkaḷ uyartiṇi āṇorumi peṇṇorumi, aḵṟiṇiyorumiyākiya mukkūṟṟorumit taṉmi viṉimuṟṟum kuṟippumuṟṟumām eṉpatu itaṉporuḷ.

	204.	avaṟṟuḷ,
ceykeṉ kiḷavi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyiṉum
avviya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.

(i-ḷ) muṟkūṟiya orumittaṉmi viṉiyēḻaṉuḷ ceyku eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu muṟṟuccol peyaroṭu muṭitalēyaṉṟi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyiṉum, muṟṟuccollām attaṉmiyil tiriyāteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar. eṉṟavāṟu.
ceyku eṉpatu viṉimutal viṉiyallatu piṟaviṉi koḷḷāmiyiṉ taṉmi yorumiyuṇarttum muṟṟuc collām taṉmiyil tiriyātu niṉṟateṉavē koḷḷappaṭum eṉpār avviyal tiriyātu eṉṟār.
(u-m) kāṇku vantēṉ eṉavarum.
kāṇku yāṉ eṉap peyaroṭu muṭital ilakkaṇam eṉṟu koḷka eṉpar teyvacciliyār.
etirkālam paṟṟi ulakavaḻakkiṟ perukappayilāta kuṭutuṟu eṉṉum vikutikaḷip piṟkūṟātu mūṉṟu kālamum paṟṟi ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peruka vaḻaṅkum eṉ ēṉ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ muṉ kum ṭum tum ṟum eṉṉum vikutikaḷuṭaṉ iyiya vittatu `ceyku eṉpatu pōlac `ceykum eṉpatum `kāṇkum vantēm eṉa viṉi koṇṭu muṭiyum eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku eṉpar cēṉāvariyar. ceykeṉ kiḷavi viṉiyoṭu muṭiyiṉum amika eṉavē ceykum eṉ kiḷaviyum viṉiyoṭu muṭiyiṉum amika eṉpatu pōntatām eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. ivvitiyiṉi,
	332.	ceyke ṉorumiyuñ ceykumeṉ paṉmiyum

	213.	atuccol vēṟṟumi yuṭimi yāṉum

ṟappāṟ kālaṅ kuṟippoṭu tōṉṟum.

(i-ḷ) āṟām vēṟṟumiyatu uṭimip poruṭ kaṇṇum ēḻām vēṟṟumiyatu nilap poruṭkaṇṇum oppiṉ kaṇṇum paṇpiṉkaṇṇum appakutikkālam kuṟippāl tōṉṟum. eṉṟavāṟu.
appakutik kālamāvatu apporuṭpakuti paṟṟi varum collakattuk kālamām. appakutikkālam kuṟippāl tōṉṟu meṉavē, apporuḷpaṟṟi viṉikkuṟippu varum eṉpatām.
atuc colvēṟṟumi - āṟām vēṟṟumi. uṭimi eṉpatu, uṭimit taṉmiyiyum uṭimip poruḷiyum kuṟikkum. `itaṉi iḵtu uṭittu eṉpatupaṭa varum ellā vāypāṭum taḻuvutaṟku atuccol vēṟṟumi yuṭimiyāṉum eṉṟār. kaṇṇeṉ vēṟṟumi nilattiṉāṉum eṉṟa toṭarkkum ivviḷakkam poruntum.
(u-m) kacciṉaṉ, kaḻaliṉaṉ eṉavum, illattaṉ, puṟattaṉ eṉavum, poṉṉaṉṉaṉ, pulipōlvaṉ eṉavum, kariyaṉ, ceyyaṉ eṉavum varum.
vaṉintāṉ eṉṉum terinili viṉimuṟṟuccol, ceytāṉ eṉṉuṅ kāriyattiṉi nikaḻttuṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷ eṭṭiṉiyum uḷḷaṭakki viṉimutalōṭu amintu māṟi niṉṟu, avāynili tōṉṟiya kālattuc ceyappaṭu poruḷ mutaliya ēḻiṉiyum virittu niṟkumāṟu pōla, uṭiyaṉ eṉṉum  viṉikkuṟippu muṟṟuc collum, uṭiyaṉā yiruntāṉ eṉa virintuḻi uṇarappaṭuṅ kāriyattiṉi nikaḻttuṅ kāraṇaṅkaḷ eṭṭiṉiyum uḷḷaṭakki viṉimutalōṭu māṟi niṉṟu, avāy nili tōṉṟiya kālattuc ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliya ēḻiṉiyum virittu niṟkumātalāṉ, uṭiyaṉ eṉpataṉuḷ aṭaṅki niṉṟa ceyappaṭuporuḷ tōṉṟak kuḻiyi eṉa urupi virittal ataṟku iyalpeṉṟu uṇarka. itteri niliyuṅ kuṟippum iṅṅaṉam urupi virittu niṟṟaliṉaṉṟē āciriyar aiyeṉap peyariya vēṟṟumik kiḷavi - evvaḻi variṉum viṉiyē viṉikkuṟippu avviru mutaliṟ ṟōṉṟu matuvē eṉṟatu. teriniliviṉit toḻiṟ peyar kālantōṉṟi niṉṟāṟpōlak kacciṉāṉ illattāṉ eṉṟāṟpōluṅ kuṟippup peyarum kālantōṉṟi niṟkum eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl, aiyāṭṭiyaṉ, tuṇaṅkiyaṉ eṉac ciṟupāṉmi kālamum viṉiceyyiṭamum paṟṟi viṉik kuṟippu varutaluṅ koḷka.
kālam kuṟippoṭu tōṉṟalāvatu, `ivaṉ tiruvuṭiyaṉ eṉṟavaḻic colvāṉ kuṟipput toṉṟutoṭṭut tiruvuṭiyaṉ eṉpatāyiṉ, iṟantakālam kāṭṭum. ippoḻutu eṉpatāyiṉ nikaḻkālam kāṭṭum. iṉi eṉpatāyiṉ etirkālam kāṭṭum eṉa viḷakkuvar teyvacciliyār.
	214.	aṉmiyi ṉiṉmiyi ṉuṇmiyiṉ vaṉmiyiṉ
aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kuṟippoṭu koḷḷum
eṉṉa kiḷaviyuṅ kuṟippē kālam.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ) aṉmi, iṉmi, uṇmi, vaṉmi eṉṉum poruḷ paṟṟi varuvaṉavum avipōlvaṉa piṟavumāyk kuṟippup poruṇmiyoṭu poruntum ellāc collum kālam kuṟippāl uṇarappaṭum collām. eṉṟavāṟu.
kālam kuṟippāl uṇarappaṭum eṉavē iviyum viṉik kuṟippām eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) allaṉ, allaḷ, allar eṉavum, ilaṉ, ilaḷ, ilar eṉavum, uḷaṉ, uḷaḷ, uḷar eṉavum, vallaṉ, vallaḷ, vallar eṉavum varum.
ivi oruvāypāṭēpaṟṟip piṟattaliṉ vēṟu kūṟiṉār. aṉṉa piṟavum eṉṟataṉāl, nallaṉ, nallaḷ, nallar; tīyaṉ, tīyaḷ, tīyar; uṭiyaṉ, uṭiyaḷ, uṭiyar eṉṉum toṭakkat taṉavum koḷka.
viṉikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷ oppuṇarttiyum paṇpuṇartti yum kuṟippuṇarttiyum uṭimikku māṟāyum varuvaṉa. oppāvatu viṉi payaṉ mey uru eṉpaṉavaṟṟāl oṉṟaṉi yoṉṟu ottal. paṇpāvatu oru poruḷ tōṉṟuṅ kālattu uṭaṉ tōṉṟi atu keṭumaḷavum niṟpatu. uṇmiyāvatu oru poruṭkuk kēṭu piṟantālum taṉakkuk kēṭiṉṟit tāṉ oṉṟēyāyp palavakippaṭṭa poruḷtōṟum niṟpatu. kuṟippāvatu poruṭkup piṉ tōṉṟic ciṟitu poḻutu nikaḻvatu.
viṉikkuṟippu iṉṉa poruḷ paṟṟi varum eṉa ivviru cūttiraṅkaḷālum āciriyar tolkāppiyar kūṟiyataṉi yuḷaṅ koṇṭa pavaṇantiyār,
	320.	poruṇmuta lāṟiṉun tōṟṟimuṉ ṉuṟaṉuḷ
viṉimutal māttiri viḷakkal viṉikkuṟippē.
eṉavarum cūttirattāl kuṟippuviṉic colliṉatu potu vilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉār.
poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉi, kuṇam, toḻil eṉṉum aṟuvakip peyarum mutaṉiliyāka avaṟṟiṉkaṇ tōṉṟi, mēl karuttā mutalākac collappaṭṭa aṟuvakip poruḷkaḷuḷ, karuttā oṉṟiyumē viḷakkutal viṉik kuṟippuc colliṉatu ilakkaṇamām eṉpatu itaṉporuḷ.
(u-m) kuḻiyiṉaṉ, akattiṉaṉ, ātiriyāṉ, kuṟuntāḷaṉ, kariyaṉ, kaṭunaṭiyaṉ eṉavarum.
ivi poruḷ iṭam kālam ciṉi kuṇam toḻil eṉpavaṟṟāl oru poruḷi vaḻaṅkutaṟkuvarum peyarāy nillātu, ivaṉ kuḻiyi yuṭiyaṉāyiṉāṉ eṉa āṟām vēṟṟumiyatu uṭimipporuṭkaṇṇum, akattiṉkaṇ iruntaṉaṉ eṉa ēḻām vēṟṟumiyatu iṭapporuṭkaṇṇum, ātiri nāḷiṟ piṟantāṉ eṉa avvēḻām vēṟṟumiyatu kālap poruṭkaṇṇum, kuṟuntāḷiyuṭiyaṉ eṉa āṟām vēṟṟumiyatu uṭimip poruṭkaṇṇum, karuvaṇṇa māyiruppaṉ eṉap paṇpiṉkaṇṇum, kaṭunaṭiyāka naṭappāṉ eṉat toḻiṟ paṇpiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ viṉikkuṟippuc collāyiṉa. ivi muṉpu viṉikkuṟippāyp piṉpu poruḷ kaḷi vaḻaṅkutaṟku varum peyarāy variṉ, kuṟippu viṉiyāla ṇiyum peyarām.
	215.	paṉmiyu morumiyum pālaṟi vanta
aṉṉa marapiṟ kuṟippoṭu varūuṅ
kālak kiḷavi yuyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ
mēlik kiḷaviyoṭu vēṟupā ṭilavē.
itu, muṟkūṟiya viṉikkuṟippiṟku īṟāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) paṉmiyum orumiyumākiya pālkaḷi aṟiyavanta attaṉmittākiya kuṟippup poruṇmi yuṭiyavāy varum viṉiccol, uyartiṇiyiṭattu mēṟkūṟiya terinili viṉiyōṭu vēṟupāṭiṉṟi oppaṉa. eṉṟavāṟu.
terinili viṉiyoṭu ottalāvatu, uyartiṇit terinili viṉikku ōtiya īṟukaḷuḷ tamakku ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu viṉik kuṟippu vantavaḻi, avvav vīṟṟāl avvappālum iṭamum viḷakkutal. mēl, viṉikkuṟippu iṉṉa poruḷpaṟṟi varum eṉṟatallatu iṉṉa īṟṟāl iṉṉapāl viḷakkum eṉak kūṟāmiyiṉ ataṉi iṅkuk kūṟiṉār āciriyar.
taṉmiyum paṭarkkiyum uṇarttum teriniliviṉi yīṟukaḷuḷ kuṟippuviṉikku ēṟpaṉa;- am, ām, em, ēm, eṉ, ēṉ eṉṉum taṉmiyīṟu āṟum, aṉ, āṉ, aḷ, āḷ, ar, ār eṉṉum paṭarkkiyīṟu āṟum eṉap paṉṉiraṇṭām.
(u-m) kariyam, kariyām, kariyem, kariyēm, kariyeṉ, kariyēṉ, e-m, kariyaṉ, kariyāṉ, kariyaḷ, kariyāḷ, kariyar, kariyār e-m, avvavvīṟu avvav iṭamum pālum viḷakkiya vāṟaṟika. oḻinta poruṭkaṇṇum oṭṭuka.
`āṉ, āḷ, ār, eṉpaṉa nilap poruṇmikkaṇ allatu piṟaporuṭkaṇ payiṉṟu vārā. vantaṉaṉ eṉat teriniliviṉi, toḻiṉmi mēṟpaṭat toḻiluṭip poruḷ kīḻppaṭa muṟṟāy niṉṟuṇarttiyavāṟu pōla, uṭiyaṉ eṉak kuṟippuviṉiyum uṭimi mēṟpaṭa uṭiyāṉ kīḻppaṭa muṟṟāy niṉṟuṇarttu taluṅ koḷka. vantāṉ, uṭiyāṉ eṉap peyarāyavaḻit toḻiluṭip poruḷum uṭiyāṉum mēṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟu māṟaṟika. iḵtu, aḵṟiṇi maruṅkiṉ - mēlik kiḷaviyoṭu vēṟupāṭilavē eṉpataṟkum okkum.
	216.	aā veṉa varūu miṟuti
appāṉ mūṉṟē palavaṟṟup paṭarkki.
itu, muṟiyāṉē aḵṟiṇit teriniliviṉiyuḷ paṉmi viṉi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) akaramum ākāramum vakara vuyirmeyyumākiya īṟṟiyuṭiya akkūṟṟu mūṉṟucollum aḵṟiṇip paṉmip paṭarkki viṉiyām.
akaram mūṉṟu kālamum paṟṟivarum. ākāram, etir maṟi viṉiyāy mūṉṟu kālattiṟkum urittāyiṉum, etir kālattup payiṉṟuvarum. akaram, iṟantakālam paṟṟi varuṅkāl kaṭataṟa eṉṉum (kālaveḻuttu) nāṉkaṉmuṉ aṉ (cāriyi) peṟṟum, peṟātum varum. ēṉi eḻuttiṉmuṉ ciṟupāṉmi iṉṉēyaṉṟi aṉpeṟṟum peṟātum varum. nikaḻkālattiṉkaṇ nil, kiṉṟu eṉpavaṟṟōṭu aṉ (cāriyi) peṟṟum peṟātum varum. etirkālattiṉkaṇ pakara vakarattōṭu aṉ (cāriyi) peṟṟum peṟātum varum eṉpar cēṉāvariyar. iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa kālaveḻuttup paṟṟiya viḷakkamum avi paṟṟiya naṉṉūlār viḷakkamum muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
(u-m) tokkaṉa, tokka; uṇṭaṉa, uṇṭa; vantaṉa, vanta; ceṉṟaṉa, ceṉṟa. e-m, añciṉa e-m, pōyiṉa, pōyaṉa, pōya e-m, uṇṇāniṉṟaṉa, uṇṇāniṉṟa: uṇkiṉṟaṉa, uṇkiṉṟa. e-m, uṇpaṉa, uṇpa; varuvaṉa, varuva. e-m, varum. variñuvaṉa variñuva eṉa ukarattōṭu ēṉiyeḻuttup pēṟum ēṟṟavaḻik koḷka.

	328.	aā vīṟṟa palaviṉ paṭarkki
āvē etirmaṟik kaṇṇa tākum.
